                    update_balance_C1()

        except Exception:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

def validate_withdrawal_C1() -> None:
    """Validate withdrawal."""
    global WS_VALID_FLAG
    WS_VALID_FLAG = 'Y'
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT, ACCOUNT_RECORD
    if WS_CALC_AMOUNT > ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_available:
        if WS_CALC_AMOUNT > (ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_available + ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_overdraft_limit):
            WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        else:
            apply_overdraft_fee_C1()

    """Apply overdraft fee."""

    WS_TOTAL_FEES += Decimal("35.00") # Assuming WS_OVERDRAFT_FEE is 35.00
    ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_balance -= Decimal("35.00") # Assuming WS_OVERDRAFT_FEE is 35.00

def determine_rate_C1() -> None:
    """Determine rate."""
    global ACCOUNT_RECORD, WS_CALC_RATE
    if ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_type == 'CH':
        WS_CALC_RATE = Decimal("0.0050") # Assuming WS_CHECKING_RATE is 0.0050
    elif ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_type == 'SV':
        WS_CALC_RATE = Decimal("0.0225") # Assuming WS_SAVINGS_RATE is 0.0225
    elif ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_type == 'MM':
        WS_CALC_RATE = Decimal("0.0350") # Assuming WS_MM_RATE is 0.0350
    elif ACCOUNT_RECORD.acct_type == 'CD':
        WS_CALC_RATE = Decimal("0.0425") # Assuming WS_CD_RATE_1YR is 0.0425
    else:
        WS_CALC_RATE = Decimal("0.00")

    """COBOL logic"""

def calculate_payment_C1() -> None:
    """Calculate payment."""
    global LOAN_RECORD, WS_CALC_PAYMENT, WS_CALC_INTEREST, WS_CALC_PRINCIPAL
    WS_CALC_PAYMENT = LOAN_RECORD.loan_payment_amount
    WS_CALC_INTEREST = LOAN_RECORD.loan_current_balance * LOAN_RECORD.loan_interest_rate / 12
    WS_CALC_PRINCIPAL = WS_CALC_PAYMENT - WS_CALC_INTEREST

def calculate_tax_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates tax."""
    global WS_CALC_TAX
    if WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_1_MAX:
        WS_CALC_TAX = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE
    elif WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_2_MAX:
        WS_CALC_TAX = (WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + ((WS_CALC_AMOUNT - WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_2_RATE)
    elif WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_3_MAX:
        WS_CALC_TAX = (WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + ((WS_BRACKET_2_MAX - WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_2_RATE) + ((WS_CALC_AMOUNT - WS_BRACKET_2_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_3_RATE)
    else:
        WS_CALC_TAX = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * WS_BRACKET_5_RATE

def calculate_risk_score_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates risk score."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT
    WS_CALC_RESULT = Decimal("0.00")
    if CUST_CREDIT_SCORE < 600:
        WS_CALC_RESULT += 30
    if CUST_TOTAL_LOANS > CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE:
        WS_CALC_RESULT += 20

def update_customer_profile_C2() -> None:
    """Updates customer profile."""
    global CUST_RISK_RATING
    if WS_CALC_RESULT > 50:
        CUST_RISK_RATING = 'H'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 25:
        CUST_RISK_RATING = 'M'
    else:
        CUST_RISK_RATING = 'L'

def dti_calculation_C2() -> None:
    """DTI calculation."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, WS_NOT_APPROVED
    WS_CALC_RESULT = LOAN_PAYMENT_AMOUNT / (CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE / 12)
    if WS_CALC_RESULT > Decimal("0.43"):
        WS_NOT_APPROVED = True

def ltv_calculation_C2() -> None:
    """LTV calculation."""
    global LOAN_LTV_RATIO, WS_CALC_FEE
    LOAN_LTV_RATIO = LOAN_CURRENT_BALANCE / LOAN_COLLATERAL_VALUE
    if LOAN_LTV_RATIO > Decimal("0.80"):
        WS_CALC_FEE += WS_LOAN_ORIGINATION_PCT

def portfolio_analysis_C2() -> None:
    """Analyzes portfolios."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    print("ANALYZING PORTFOLIOS...")
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        # READ investment_master NEXT
        #     AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
        #     NOT AT END
        #         PERFORM 7911-calculate_returns
        #         PERFORM 7912-assess_risk
        #         PERFORM 7913-benchmark_comparison
        # 
        WS_EOF = True # Replace with actual file reading logic
        if not WS_EOF:
            calculate_returns_C2()
            assess_risk_C2()
            benchmark_comparison_C2()

def calculate_returns_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates returns."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT
    if INV_PURCHASE_PRICE > 0:
        WS_CALC_RESULT = (INV_CURRENT_PRICE - INV_PURCHASE_PRICE) / INV_PURCHASE_PRICE * 100

    """Assess risk."""

        WS_TEMP_FLAG = 'H'

        WS_TEMP_FLAG = 'L'
    elif INV_MUTUAL_FUND:
        WS_TEMP_FLAG = 'M'
    else:
        WS_TEMP_FLAG = 'M'

def customer_segmentation_C3() -> None:
    """Segments customers."""
    print("SEGMENTING CUSTOMERS...")
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        # Simulate reading from customer_master
        # Replace this with actual file reading logic
        # For example:
        # try:
        #     customer_record = next(customer_file_iterator)
        # except StopIteration:
        #     WS_EOF = True
        #     break

        # Simulate reading customer_master NEXT
        customer_record = None  # Replace with actual customer record if available
        if customer_record is None:  # Simulate AT END
            WS_EOF = True
        else:  # Simulate NOT AT END
            calculate_clv_C3()
            assign_segment_C3()

def calculate_clv_C3() -> None:
    """Calculates customer lifetime value."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT
    WS_CALC_RESULT = (CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_balance * WS_SAVINGS_RATE) + \
                      (CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_loans * WS_PERSONAL_RATE) + \
                      (CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_investments * Decimal("0.01"))

def assign_segment_C3() -> None:
    """Assigns a segment to a customer."""
    global WS_TEMP_CODE
    if WS_CALC_RESULT > 10000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'PLATINUM'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 5000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'GOLD'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 1000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'SILVER'
    else:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'BRONZE'

def sweep_accounts_C3() -> None:
    """Manages sweep accounts."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT, WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS, ACCT_BALANCE
    if ACCT_BALANCE > ACCT_MIN_BALANCE:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = ACCT_BALANCE - ACCT_MIN_BALANCE
        ACCT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT
        WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS += None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT

def extract_data_C3() -> None:
    """Extracts data."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF, WS_PROCESS_COUNT
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        # Simulate reading from customer_master
        # Replace this with actual file reading logic
        # For example:
        # try:
        #     customer_record = next(customer_file_iterator)
        # except StopIteration:
        #     WS_EOF = True
        #     break

        # Simulate reading customer_master NEXT
        customer_record = None  # Replace with actual customer record if available
        if customer_record is None:  # Simulate AT END
            WS_EOF = True
        else:  # Simulate NOT AT END
            WS_PROCESS_COUNT += 1

        WS_ERROR_COUNT += 1

    """Checks for timeliness."""
    global WS_ERROR_COUNT
    if 'CUST_LAST_ACTIVITY' in locals() and (CUST_LAST_ACTIVITY < WS_CURRENT_DATE - 365):
        WS_ERROR_COUNT += 1

@dataclass
class DataItemC4:
    """Data item structure."""
    cust_status: Optional[str] = None
    cust_ssn: Optional[str] = None
    ws_temp_code: Optional[str] = None
    ws_calc_result: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_total_deposits: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_total_loans: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_calc_amount: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_total_fees: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_not_eof: Optional[bool] = None
    ws_eof: Optional[bool] = None
    transaction_log: Optional[str] = None
    tran_amount: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_process_count: Optional[int] = None
    ws_error_count: Optional[int] = None
    cust_credit_score: Optional[int] = None
    cust_risk_rating: Optional[str] = None
    cust_total_balance: Optional[Decimal] = None
    cust_total_loans: Optional[Decimal] = None
    loan_current_balance: Optional[Decimal] = None
    loan_paid_off: Optional[bool] = None
    ws_atm_fee_foreign: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_current_timestamp: Optional[str] = None
    ws_temp_string: Optional[str] = None
    ws_valid: Optional[bool] = None
    ws_formatted_count: Optional[str] = None
    ws_cust_count: Optional[int] = None

data_item_C4 = DataItemC4()

def c100_transaction_monitoring_C4() -> None:
    """Transaction monitoring."""
    print("MONITORING TRANSACTIONS...")
    data_item_C4.ws_not_eof = True
    while not data_item_C4.ws_eof:
        # Simulate READ transaction_log NEXT AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
        # NOT AT END PERFORM C110-rule_based_detection PERFORM C120-behavior_analysis PERFORM C130-network_analysis
        data_item_C4.transaction_log = None  # Assuming read failure sets transaction_log to None
        if data_item_C4.transaction_log is None:
            data_item_C4.ws_eof = True
        else:
            c110_rule_based_detection_C4()
            c120_behavior_analysis_C4()
            c130_network_analysis_C4()
    pass

def c110_rule_based_detection_C4() -> None:
    """Rule-based detection."""
    if (data_item_C4.tran_amount or Decimal('0')) >= 10000:
        c111_flag_ctr_C4()
    if 5000 <= (data_item_C4.tran_amount or Decimal('0')) < 10000:
        c112_check_structuring_C4()
    pass

def c111_flag_ctr_C4() -> None:
    """Flag CTR."""
    data_item_C4.ws_process_count = (data_item_C4.ws_process_count or 0) + 1
    pass

def c112_check_structuring_C4() -> None:
    """Check structuring."""
    data_item_C4.ws_error_count = (data_item_C4.ws_error_count or 0) + 1
    pass

def d110_classification_C4() -> None:
    """Classification."""
    if (data_item_C4.cust_credit_score or 0) > 750:
        data_item_C4.cust_risk_rating = 'A'
    elif (data_item_C4.cust_credit_score or 0) > 650:
        data_item_C4.cust_risk_rating = 'B'
    elif (data_item_C4.cust_credit_score or 0) > 550:
        data_item_C4.cust_risk_rating = 'C'
    else:
        data_item_C4.cust_risk_rating = 'D'
    pass

def d120_regression_C4() -> None:
    """Regression."""
    data_item_C4.ws_calc_result = ((data_item_C4.cust_credit_score or 0) * 10) + \
        ((data_item_C4.cust_total_balance or Decimal('0')) / 1000) - \
        ((data_item_C4.cust_total_loans or Decimal('0')) / 2000)
    pass

def initialize_tables_1300_C5() -> None:
    """Initialize table entries."""
    ws_tbl_idx: int = 0
    rate_table_entry: RateTableEntryC5 = RateTableEntryC5()
    branch_table_entry: BranchTableEntryC5 = BranchTableEntryC5()
    rt_rate: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    rt_code: str = ""
    for ws_tbl_idx in range(1, 101):
        rt_rate = Decimal("0.00")
        rt_code = " "
    for ws_tbl_idx in range(1, 51):
        pass
    pass

def load_reference_data_1400_C5() -> None:
    """Load reference data from file."""
    ws_tbl_idx: int = 0
    ws_eof_flag: str = ""
    ws_ref_record: ReferenceRecordC5 = ReferenceRecordC5()
    ws_ref_code: str = ""
    ws_ref_rate: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    rt_code: str = ""
    rt_rate: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_tbl_idx = 1
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y' and ws_tbl_idx <= 100:
        reference_file: str = ""
        if reference_file:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:
            ws_ref_code = ""
            ws_ref_rate = Decimal("0.00")
            rt_code = ws_ref_code
            rt_rate = ws_ref_rate
            ws_tbl_idx += 1
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    pass

def process_transactions_2000_C5() -> None:
    """Process transaction records."""
    ws_transaction_rec: WsTransactionRecC5 = WsTransactionRecC5()
    ws_eof_flag: str = ""
    ws_trans_count: int = 0
    ws_valid_flag: str = ""
    txn_account_id: str = ""
    txn_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    txn_type: str = ""
    transaction_file: str = ""
    if transaction_file:
        ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_trans_count += 1
        validate_transaction_2100_C5()
        if ws_valid_flag == 'Y':
            process_by_type_2200_C5()
        else:
            handle_error_2900_C5()
    pass

def validate_transaction_2100_C5() -> None:
    """Validate transaction data."""
    ws_valid_flag: str = ""
    txn_account_id: str = ""
    txn_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    txn_type: str = ""
    ws_error_msg: str = ""
    ws_valid_flag = 'Y'
    if txn_account_id == " ":
        ws_valid_flag = 'N'
        ws_error_msg = 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID'
        return
    if not isinstance(txn_amount, Decimal):
        ws_valid_flag = 'N'
        ws_error_msg = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
        return
    if txn_type not in ['D', 'W', 'T', 'I']:
        ws_valid_flag = 'N'
        ws_error_msg = 'INVALID TRANSACTION TYPE'
    validate_account_exists_2150_C5()
    validate_business_rules_2160_C5()
    pass

def validate_account_exists_2150_C5() -> None:
    """Validate account existence."""
    txn_account_id: str = ""
    ws_search_key: str = ""
    ws_found_flag: str = ""
    ws_error_msg: str = ""
    ws_search_key = txn_account_id
    search_account_5000_C5()
    if ws_found_flag == 'N':
        ws_valid_flag: str = ""
        ws_valid_flag = 'N'
        ws_error_msg = 'ACCOUNT NOT FOUND'
    pass

def validate_business_rules_2160_C5() -> None:
    """Validate business rules for transactions."""
    txn_type: str = ""
    txn_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_account_balance: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_valid_flag: str = ""
    ws_error_msg: str = ""
    if txn_type == 'W':
        if txn_amount > ws_account_balance:
            ws_valid_flag = 'N'
            ws_error_msg = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
    if txn_amount > Decimal("1000000.00"):
        ws_valid_flag = 'N'
        ws_error_msg = 'AMOUNT EXCEEDS LIMIT'
    pass

def process_by_type_2200_C5() -> None:
    """Process transaction by type."""
    txn_type: str = ""
    if txn_type == 'D':
        process_deposit_2300_C5()
    elif txn_type == 'W':
        process_withdrawal_2400_C5()
    elif txn_type == 'T':
        process_transfer_2500_C5()
    elif txn_type == 'I':
        process_interest_2600_C5()
    else:
        handle_error_2900_C5()
    pass

def process_deposit_2300_C5() -> None:
    """Process deposit transaction."""
    txn_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_account_balance: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_txn_desc: str = ""
    ws_total_deposits: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_deposit_count: int = 0
    ws_account_balance += txn_amount
    ws_txn_desc = 'DEPOSIT'
    ws_total_deposits += txn_amount
    ws_deposit_count += 1
    update_account_2350_C5()
    write_audit_trail_2380_C5()
    pass

    ws_error_msg: str = ""
    acct_balance: Decimal = ws_account_balance

    if ws_file_status != '00':
        ws_error_msg = 'UPDATE FAILED'

    pass

def process_withdrawal_2400_C5() -> None:
    """Process withdrawal transaction."""
    txn_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_account_balance: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_txn_desc: str = ""
    ws_total_withdrawals: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_withdrawal_count: int = 0
    ws_min_balance_limit: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_account_balance -= txn_amount
    ws_txn_desc = 'WITHDRAWAL'
    ws_total_withdrawals += txn_amount
    ws_withdrawal_count += 1
    update_account_2350_C5()
    write_audit_trail_2380_C5()
    if ws_account_balance < ws_min_balance_limit:
        generate_low_balance_alert_2450_C5()
    pass

    ws_alert_count += 1
    pass

    pass

    pass

    pass

def process_interest_2600_C5() -> None:
    """Process interest payment."""
    ws_account_balance: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_interest_rate: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_txn_desc: str = ""
    ws_total_interest: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_interest_count: int = 0
    ws_interest_amount: Decimal = ws_account_balance * ws_interest_rate / 100
    ws_account_balance += ws_interest_amount
    ws_txn_desc = 'INTEREST'
    ws_total_interest += ws_interest_amount
    ws_interest_count += 1
    update_account_2350_C5()
    write_audit_trail_2380_C5()
    pass

def handle_error_2900_C5() -> None:
    """Handle error conditions."""
    txn_account_id: str = ""
    ws_error_msg: str = ""
    ws_error_count: int = 0
    ws_max_errors: int = 0
    ws_abort_reason: str = ""
    ws_error_record: ErrorRecordC5 = ErrorRecordC5()
    err_account: str = txn_account_id
    err_message: str = ws_error_msg
    err_timestamp: str = ""
    ws_error_count += 1
    if ws_error_count > ws_max_errors:
        ws_abort_reason = 'MAX ERRORS EXCEEDED'
        abort_process_9500_C5()
    pass

def abort_process_9500_C5() -> None:
    """Placeholder for abort_process_9500."""
    pass

def batch_processing_3000_C5() -> None:
    """Process a batch of items."""
    load_batch_header_3100_C5()
    process_batch_items_3200_C5()
    ws_batch_eof: str = ""
    while ws_batch_eof != 'Y':
        process_batch_items_3200_C5()
    validate_batch_totals_3300_C5()
    commit_batch_3400_C5()
    pass

def load_batch_header_3100_C5() -> None:
    """Load batch header information."""
    ws_batch_header: BatchHeaderC5 = BatchHeaderC5()
    batch_id: str = ""
    batch_count: int = 0
    batch_total: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_batch_eof: str = ""
    ws_current_batch: str = ""
    ws_expected_count: int = 0
    ws_expected_total: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    batch_file: str = ""
    if batch_file:
        ws_batch_eof = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_current_batch = batch_id
        ws_expected_count = batch_count
        ws_expected_total = batch_total
    pass

def process_batch_items_3200_C5() -> None:
    """Process individual items within a batch."""
    ws_batch_item: BatchItemC5 = BatchItemC5()
    item_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    item_type: str = ""
    item_account: str = ""
    ws_batch_eof: str = ""
    ws_actual_count: int = 0
    ws_actual_total: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    batch_file: str = ""
    if batch_file:
        ws_batch_eof = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_actual_count += 1
        ws_actual_total += item_amount
        process_single_item_3250_C5()
    pass

def process_single_item_3250_C5() -> None:
    """Process a single item based on its type."""
    item_type: str = ""
    if item_type == 'PAY':
        process_payment_3260_C5()
    elif item_type == 'REF':
        process_refund_3270_C5()
    elif item_type == 'ADJ':
        process_adjustment_3280_C5()
    pass

def process_payment_3260_C5() -> None:
    """Process a payment item."""
    item_account: str = ""
    ws_search_key: str = ""
    ws_found_flag: str = ""
    item_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_payment_count: int = 0
    ws_search_key = item_account
    search_account_5000_C5()
    if ws_found_flag == 'Y':
        ws_account_balance: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        ws_account_balance -= item_amount
        update_account_2350_C5()
        ws_payment_count += 1
    pass

def process_refund_3270_C5() -> None:
    """Process a refund item."""
    item_account: str = ""
    ws_search_key: str = ""
    ws_found_flag: str = ""
    item_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_refund_count: int = 0
    ws_search_key = item_account
    search_account_5000_C5()
    if ws_found_flag == 'Y':
        ws_account_balance: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        ws_account_balance += item_amount
        update_account_2350_C5()
        ws_refund_count += 1
    pass

def process_adjustment_3280_C5() -> None:
    """Process an adjustment item."""
    item_account: str = ""
    ws_search_key: str = ""
    ws_found_flag: str = ""
    item_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_adjustment_count: int = 0
    ws_search_key = item_account
    search_account_5000_C5()
    if ws_found_flag == 'Y':
        ws_account_balance: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        if item_amount > Decimal("0.00"):
            ws_account_balance += item_amount
        else:
            ws_account_balance -= item_amount
        update_account_2350_C5()
        ws_adjustment_count += 1
    pass

def validate_batch_totals_3300_C5() -> None:
    """Validate batch totals against expected values."""
    ws_actual_count: int = 0
    ws_expected_count: int = 0
    ws_actual_total: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_expected_total: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_error_msg: str = ""
    if ws_actual_count != ws_expected_count:
        ws_error_msg = 'BATCH COUNT MISMATCH'
        reject_batch_3350_C5()
    if ws_actual_total != ws_expected_total:
        ws_error_msg = 'BATCH TOTAL MISMATCH'
        reject_batch_3350_C5()
    pass

def reject_batch_3350_C5() -> None:
    """Reject a batch due to validation failure."""
    ws_current_batch: str = ""
    ws_error_msg: str = ""
    ws_rejection_record: RejectionRecordC5 = RejectionRecordC5()
    ws_rejected_batch_count: int = 0
    rej_batch_id: str = ws_current_batch
    rej_reason: str = ws_error_msg
    rej_date: str = ""
    ws_rejected_batch_count += 1
    pass

def commit_batch_3400_C5() -> None:
    """Commit a batch after successful validation."""
    ws_batch_valid: str = ""
    if ws_batch_valid == 'Y':
        ws_committed_batch_count: int = 0
        ws_committed_batch_count += 1
        update_batch_status_3450_C5()
    pass

def update_batch_status_3450_C5() -> None:
    """Update the batch status after successful commit."""
    batch_status: str = ""
    batch_commit_date: str = ""
    batch_header_record: BatchHeaderC5 = BatchHeaderC5()
    batch_status = 'COMMITTED'
    pass

def write_daily_details_4150_C5() -> None:
    """Write the details section of the daily report."""
    ws_trans_count: int = 0
    ws_total_deposits: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_total_withdrawals: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_total_transfers: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

    rpt_deposits: Decimal = ws_total_deposits
    rpt_withdrawals: Decimal = ws_total_withdrawals
    rpt_transfers: Decimal = ws_total_transfers
    rpt_net_amount: Decimal = ws_total_deposits - ws_total_withdrawals
    ws_report_detail: ReportRecordC5 = ReportRecordC5()
    pass

def set_interest_rate_C6(case: str) -> None:
    """Set interest rate based on case."""
    global WS_INTEREST_RATE_C6
    if case == "case1":
        WS_INTEREST_RATE_C6 = 2.0
    else:
        WS_INTEREST_RATE_C6 = 2.5

def calculate_simple_interest_C6() -> None:
    """Calculate simple interest."""
    global WS_SIMPLE_INTEREST_C6
    WS_SIMPLE_INTEREST_C6 = (
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6 * WS_INTEREST_RATE_C6 * WS_DAYS_IN_PERIOD_C6 / 36500
    )

def calculate_compound_interest_C6() -> None:
    """Calculate compound interest."""
    global WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR_C6, WS_COMPOUND_INTEREST_C6
    WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR_C6 = (
        1 + WS_INTEREST_RATE_C6 / 36500
    ) ** WS_DAYS_IN_PERIOD_C6
    WS_COMPOUND_INTEREST_C6 = (
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6 * (WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR_C6 - 1)
    )

def apply_interest_C6() -> None:
    """Apply interest to account balance."""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6
    if WS_INTEREST_METHOD_C6 == "S":
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6 += WS_SIMPLE_INTEREST_C6
    else:
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6 += WS_COMPOUND_INTEREST_C6
    update_account_C6()

def fee_processing_C6() -> None:
    """Process fees."""
    calculate_monthly_fee_C6()
    calculate_transaction_fees_C6()
    apply_fee_waivers_C6()
    deduct_fees_C6()

def calculate_monthly_fee_C6() -> None:
    """Calculate monthly fee based on account type."""
    global WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6
    if WS_ACCOUNT_TYPE_C6 == "CHK":
        WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6 = Decimal("12.00")
    elif WS_ACCOUNT_TYPE_C6 == "SAV":
        WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6 = Decimal("5.00")
    elif WS_ACCOUNT_TYPE_C6 == "PRM":
        WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6 = Decimal("25.00")
    else:
        WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6 = Decimal("0.00")

def calculate_transaction_fees_C6() -> None:
    """Calculate transaction fees."""
    global WS_TRANS_FEE_C6, WS_EXCESS_TRANS_C6
    if WS_TRANS_COUNT_C6 > WS_FREE_TRANS_LIMIT_C6:
        WS_EXCESS_TRANS_C6 = WS_TRANS_COUNT_C6 - WS_FREE_TRANS_LIMIT_C6
        WS_TRANS_FEE_C6 = WS_EXCESS_TRANS_C6 * WS_PER_TRANS_FEE_C6
    else:
        WS_TRANS_FEE_C6 = Decimal("0.00")

def apply_fee_waivers_C6() -> None:
    """Apply fee waivers."""
    global WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6, WS_TRANS_FEE_C6
    if WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6 >= WS_MIN_BALANCE_WAIVER_C6:
        WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6 = Decimal("0.00")
    if WS_CUSTOMER_TIER_C6 == "GOLD" or WS_CUSTOMER_TIER_C6 == "PLATINUM":
        WS_TRANS_FEE_C6 = WS_TRANS_FEE_C6 * Decimal("0.5")

def deduct_fees_C6() -> None:
    """Deduct fees from account balance."""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6, WS_TOTAL_FEES_C6
    WS_TOTAL_FEES_C6 = WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6 + WS_TRANS_FEE_C6
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6 -= None  # TODO: was WS_TOTAL_FEES_C6
    update_account_C6()
    record_fee_transaction_C6()

def record_fee_transaction_C6() -> None:

def loan_processing_C6() -> None:
    """Process loan application."""
    validate_loan_application_C6()
    if WS_VALID_FLAG_C6 == "Y":
        calculate_credit_score_C6()
        assess_risk_C6()
        determine_approval_C6()
        if WS_APPROVAL_STATUS_C6 == "A":
            generate_loan_terms_C6()
            create_amortization_C6()
            finalize_loan_C6()
        else:
            process_decline_C6()

def validate_loan_application_C6() -> None:
    """Validate loan application."""
    global WS_VALID_FLAG_C6, WS_ERROR_MSG_C6
    WS_VALID_FLAG_C6 = "Y"
    if WsLoanProcessingAreaC6.ws_loan_amount < 1000:
        WS_VALID_FLAG_C6 = "N"
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C6 = "MINIMUM LOAN AMOUNT IS $1000"
        return
    if WsLoanProcessingAreaC6.ws_loan_amount > 10000000:
        WS_VALID_FLAG_C6 = "N"
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C6 = "MAXIMUM LOAN AMOUNT EXCEEDED"
        return
    if WsLoanProcessingAreaC6.ws_loan_term_months < 6 or WsLoanProcessingAreaC6.ws_loan_term_months > 360:
        WS_VALID_FLAG_C6 = "N"
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C6 = "INVALID LOAN TERM"

def calculate_credit_score_C6() -> None:
    """Calculate credit score."""
    global WS_CREDIT_SCORE_C6
    WS_CREDIT_SCORE_C6 = 0
    score_payment_history_C6()
    score_credit_utilization_C6()
    score_credit_length_C6()
    score_new_credit_C6()
    score_credit_mix_C6()
    determine_tier_C6()

def score_payment_history_C6() -> None:
    """Score payment history."""
    global WS_PAYMENT_SCORE_C6, WS_CREDIT_SCORE_C6
    WS_PAYMENT_SCORE_C6 = (
        (WsPaymentHistoryC6.ws_on_time_payments * 100) / 0  # TODO
        (WsPaymentHistoryC6.ws_on_time_payments + WsPaymentHistoryC6.ws_late_30_days + 0  # TODO
         WsPaymentHistoryC6.ws_late_60_days + WsPaymentHistoryC6.ws_late_90_days)
    )
    WS_PAYMENT_SCORE_C6 = WS_PAYMENT_SCORE_C6 * Decimal("0.35")
    WS_CREDIT_SCORE_C6 += int(WS_PAYMENT_SCORE_C6)

def score_credit_utilization_C6() -> None:
    """Score credit utilization."""
    global WS_UTIL_SCORE_C6, WS_CREDIT_SCORE_C6
    if WsCreditScoringAreaC6.ws_credit_utilization <= 10:
        WS_UTIL_SCORE_C6 = 100
    elif WsCreditScoringAreaC6.ws_credit_utilization <= 30:
        WS_UTIL_SCORE_C6 =None  # TODO: Add value

def calculate_pmi_C7(ws_ltv_ratio: Decimal, ws_loan_amount: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """Calculates PMI amount."""
    if ws_ltv_ratio > 95:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0125') / 12
    elif ws_ltv_ratio > 90:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0100') / 12
    elif ws_ltv_ratio > 85:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0075') / 12
    else:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0050') / 12
    return ws_pmi_amount

def evaluate_history_C7(ws_late_90_days: int, ws_late_60_days: int, ws_late_30_days: int, ws_risk_score: int) -> tuple[int, str, str, str]:
    """Evaluates delinquency history."""
    ws_factor_1: str = ""
    ws_factor_2: str = ""
    ws_factor_3: str = ""
    if ws_late_90_days > 0:
        ws_risk_score -= 50
        ws_factor_1 = 'SEVERE DELINQUENCY HISTORY'
    if ws_late_60_days > 2:
        ws_risk_score -= 30
        ws_factor_2 = '60+ DAY DELINQUENCIES'
    if ws_late_30_days > 5:
        ws_risk_score -= 20
        ws_factor_3 = 'MULTIPLE 30-DAY LATES'
    return ws_risk_score, ws_factor_1, ws_factor_2, ws_factor_3

def calculate_final_risk_C7(ws_risk_score: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, str]:
    """Calculates the final risk category."""
    ws_risk_score = ws_risk_score / 4
    ws_risk_category: str = ""
    if ws_risk_score >= 80:
        ws_risk_category = 'LOW RISK'
    elif ws_risk_score >= 60:
        ws_risk_category = 'MODERATE'
    elif ws_risk_score >= 40:
        ws_risk_category = 'ELEVATED'
    else:
        ws_risk_category = 'HIGH RISK'
    return ws_risk_score, ws_risk_category

def determine_approval_C7(ws_credit_tier: str, ws_risk_category: str, ws_dti_ratio: Decimal, ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_base_rate: Decimal) -> tuple[str, str, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Determines loan approval status."""
    ws_approval_status: str = ""
    ws_conditions: str = ""
    ws_approved_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_approved_rate: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    if ws_credit_tier == 'F':
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'CREDIT SCORE TOO LOW'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate
    if ws_risk_category == 'HIGH RISK':
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'RISK ASSESSMENT FAILED'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate
    if ws_dti_ratio > 50:
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'DTI RATIO TOO HIGH'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate
    ws_approval_status = 'A'
    ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate = calculate_approved_terms_C7(ws_loan_amount, ws_base_rate, ws_credit_tier, ws_risk_category)
    return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate

def calculate_approved_terms_C7(ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_base_rate: Decimal, ws_credit_tier: str, ws_risk_category: str) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Calculates approved loan terms."""
    ws_approved_amount: Decimal = ws_loan_amount
    ws_approved_rate: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    if ws_credit_tier == 'A':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('0.00')
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'B':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('0.50')
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'C':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('1.50')
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'D':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('3.00')
    if ws_risk_category == 'ELEVATED':
        ws_approved_rate += Decimal('0.50')
    return ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate

def generate_loan_terms_C7(ws_approved_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_term_months: int, ws_loan_amount: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Generates loan terms."""
    ws_loan_interest_rate: Decimal = ws_approved_rate
    ws_monthly_rate: Decimal = ws_loan_interest_rate / 1200
    ws_compound_factor: Decimal = (1 + ws_monthly_rate) ** ws_loan_term_months
    ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal = ws_loan_amount * ws_monthly_rate * ws_compound_factor / (ws_compound_factor - 1)
    ws_loan_principal_bal: Decimal = ws_loan_amount
    return ws_loan_interest_rate, ws_monthly_rate, ws_compound_factor, ws_loan_monthly_pmt

def create_amortization_C7(ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_loan_term_months: int, ws_monthly_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal, loan_mortgage: bool, ws_property_tax: Decimal, ws_insurance_premium: Decimal, ws_pmi_amount: Decimal) -> tuple[List[Decimal], List[Decimal], List[Decimal], List[int], List[Decimal], List[Decimal]]:
    """Creates an amortization schedule."""
    ws_running_balance: Decimal = ws_loan_amount
    ws_payment_date: int = 20240101
    ws_payment_month: int = 1
    ws_payment_year: int = 2024

    amort_interest: List[Decimal] = [Decimal("0.00")] * (ws_loan_term_months + 1)
    amort_principal: List[Decimal] = [Decimal("0.00")] * (ws_loan_term_months + 1)
    amort_balance: List[Decimal] = [Decimal("0.00")] * (ws_loan_term_months + 1)
    amort_payment_num: List[int] = [0] * (ws_loan_term_months + 1)
    amort_payment_amt: List[Decimal] = [Decimal("0.00")] * (ws_loan_term_months + 1)
    amort_escrow: List[Decimal] = [Decimal("0.00")] * (ws_loan_term_months + 1)
    amort_total_pmt: List[Decimal] = [Decimal("0.00")] * (ws_loan_term_months + 1)

    for ws_amort_idx in range(1, ws_loan_term_months + 1):
        amort_interest[ws_amort_idx] = ws_running_balance * ws_monthly_rate
        amort_principal[ws_amort_idx] = ws_loan_monthly_pmt - amort_interest[ws_amort_idx]
        ws_running_balance -= amort_principal[ws_amort_idx]
        amort_balance[ws_amort_idx] = ws_running_balance
        amort_payment_num[ws_amort_idx] = ws_amort_idx
        amort_payment_amt[ws_amort_idx] = ws_loan_monthly_pmt
        if loan_mortgage:
            amort_escrow[ws_amort_idx] = (ws_property_tax + ws_insurance_premium) / 12
            amort_total_pmt[ws_amort_idx] = ws_loan_monthly_pmt + amort_escrow[ws_amort_idx] + ws_pmi_amount
        else:
            amort_total_pmt[ws_amort_idx] = ws_loan_monthly_pmt
        ws_payment_month += 1
        if ws_payment_month > 12:
            ws_payment_month = 1
            ws_payment_year += 1
        amort_payment_date: int = ws_payment_year * 10000 + ws_payment_month * 100 + 1

    return amort_interest, amort_principal, amort_balance, amort_payment_num, amort_payment_amt, amort_total_pmt

def finalize_loan_C7(ws_loan_term_months: int) -> tuple[int, str]:
    """Finalizes the loan."""
    ws_loan_start_date: int = 20240101

def load_portfolio_C7() -> tuple[str, int]:
    """Loads the investment portfolio."""
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'
    ws_hold_idx: int = 1
    while ws_hold_idx <= 100 and ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        ws_holding_rec: str = read_holdings_file_C7()
        if ws_holding_rec == "EOF":
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:
            pass
            ws_hold_idx += 1
    ws_holdings_count: int = ws_hold_idx - 1
    return ws_eof_flag, ws_holdings_count

def read_holdings_file_C7() -> str:
    """Reads the holdings file (dummy)."""
    return "EOF"

def update_market_prices_C7(ws_holdings_count: int) -> None:
    """Updates the market prices of holdings."""
    for ws_hold_idx in range(1, ws_holdings_count + 1):
        ws_quote_symbol: str = get_hold_symbol_C7(ws_hold_idx)
        ws_quote_price: Decimal = get_quote_C7(ws_quote_symbol)
        set_hold_current_price_C7(ws_hold_idx, ws_quote_price)

def get_hold_symbol_C7(ws_hold_idx: int) -> str:
    """Gets the holding symbol."""
    return "DUMMY"

def set_hold_current_price_C7(ws_hold_idx: int, ws_quote_price: Decimal) -> None:
    """Sets the current price of a holding."""
    pass

def get_quote_C7(ws_quote_symbol: str) -> Decimal:
    """Gets the market quote for a symbol."""
    quote_request: str = ws_quote_symbol
    quote_response: tuple[str, Decimal] = getquote_C7(quote_request)
    quote_response_status: str = quote_response[0]
    ws_quote_price: Decimal
    if quote_response_status == 'OK':
        ws_quote_price = quote_response[1]
    else:
        ws_quote_price = Decimal("0.00")
    return ws_quote_price

def getquote_C7(quote_request: str) -> tuple[str, Decimal]:
    """Dummy function to simulate getting a quote."""
    return "OK", Decimal("100.00")

def calculate_values_C7(ws_holdings_count: int) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Calculates portfolio values."""
    ws_total_value: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_cost_basis: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_unrealized_gain: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    for ws_hold_idx in range(1, ws_holdings_count + 1):
        hold_market_value, ws_hold_cost, hold_gain_loss = calculate_holding_value_C7(ws_hold_idx)
        ws_total_value += hold_market_value
        ws_cost_basis += ws_hold_cost
        ws_unrealized_gain += hold_gain_loss
    return ws_total_value, ws_cost_basis, ws_unrealized_gain

def calculate_holding_value_C7(ws_hold_idx: int) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Calculates the value of a single holding."""
    hold_shares: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")
    hold_current_price: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")
    hold_cost_per_share: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")
    hold_market_value: Decimal = hold_shares * hold_current_price
    ws_hold_cost: Decimal = hold_shares * hold_cost_per_share
    hold_gain_loss: Decimal = hold_market_value - ws_hold_cost
    if ws_hold_cost > 0:
        hold_pct_change: Decimal = (hold_gain_loss / ws_hold_cost) * 100
    else:
        hold_pct_change = Decimal("0.00")
    return hold_market_value, ws_hold_cost, hold_gain_loss

def rebalance_check_C7(ws_total_value: Decimal, ws_target_stocks_pct: Decimal, ws_target_bonds_pct: Decimal) -> None:
    """Checks if portfolio rebalancing is needed."""
    ws_rebalance_needed, ws_stocks_pct, ws_bonds_pct = calculate_current_allocation_C7()
    ws_rebalance_needed = compare_to_target_C7(ws_stocks_pct, ws_bonds_pct, ws_target_stocks_pct, ws_target_bonds_pct)
    if ws_rebalance_needed == 'Y':
        generate_rebalance_trades_C7(ws_total_value, ws_stocks_pct, ws_target_stocks_pct)

def calculate_current_allocation_C7() -> tuple[str, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Calculates the current asset allocation."""
    ws_stocks_value: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_bonds_value: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_cash_value: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_holdings_count: int = 1
    for ws_hold_idx in range(1, ws_holdings_count + 1):
        hold_type: str = "STK" #Dummy value
        hold_market_value: Decimal = Decimal("100.00") #Dummy value
        if hold_type == 'STK':
            ws_stocks_value += hold_market_value
        elif hold_type == 'BND':
            ws_bonds_value += hold_market_value
        elif hold_type == 'CSH':
            ws_cash_value += hold_market_value
    ws_total_value: Decimal = ws_stocks_value + ws_bonds_value + ws_cash_value
    ws_stocks_pct: Decimal = (ws_stocks_value / ws_total_value) * 100
    ws_bonds_pct: Decimal = (ws_bonds_value / ws_total_value) * 100
    ws_cash_pct: Decimal = (ws_cash_value / ws_total_value) * 100
    ws_rebalance_needed: str = 'N'
    return ws_rebalance_needed, ws_stocks_pct, ws_bonds_pct

def compare_to_target_C7(ws_stocks_pct: Decimal, ws_bonds_pct: Decimal, ws_target_stocks_pct: Decimal, ws_target_bonds_pct: Decimal) -> str:
    """Compares current allocation to target."""
    ws_rebalance_needed: str = 'N'
    ws_stocks_diff: Decimal = ws_stocks_pct - ws_target_stocks_pct
    ws_bonds_diff: Decimal = ws_bonds_pct - ws_target_bonds_pct
    if abs(ws_stocks_diff) > 5:
        ws_rebalance_needed = 'Y'
    if abs(ws_bonds_diff) > 5:
        ws_rebalance_needed = 'Y'
    return ws_rebalance_needed

def generate_rebalance_trades_C7(ws_total_value: Decimal, ws_stocks_pct: Decimal, ws_target_stocks_pct: Decimal) -> None:
    """Generates trades to rebalance the portfolio."""
    ws_stocks_diff: Decimal = ws_stocks_pct - ws_target_stocks_pct
    if ws_stocks_diff > 0:
        ws_sell_amount: Decimal = ws_total_value * ws_stocks_diff / 100
        create_sell_order_C7(ws_sell_amount)
    else:
        ws_buy_amount: Decimal = ws_total_value * (0 - ws_stocks_diff) / 100
        create_buy_order_C7(ws_buy_amount)

def create_sell_order_C7(ws_sell_amount: Decimal) -> None:
    """Creates a sell order."""
    ws_trade_type: str = 'SELL'
    ws_order_type: str = 'MARKET'
    ws_trade_amount: Decimal = ws_sell_amount
    trade_execution_C7()

def create_buy_order_C7(ws_buy_amount: Decimal) -> None:
    """Creates a buy order."""
    ws_trade_type: str = 'BUY '
    ws_order_type: str = 'MARKET'
    ws_trade_amount: Decimal = ws_buy_amount
    trade_execution_C7()

def generate_statements_C7() -> None:
    """Generates investment statements."""
    monthly_statement_C7()
    ws_end_of_quarter: str = 'Y'
    if ws_end_of_quarter == 'Y':
        quarterly_report_C7()
    ws_end_of_year: str = 'Y'
    if ws_end_of_year == 'Y':
        annual_tax_report_C7()

def monthly_statement_C7() -> None:
    """Generates a monthly investment statement."""
    rpt_title: str = 'MONTHLY INVESTMENT STATEMENT'
    write_holdings_detail_C7()

def write_holdings_detail_C7() -> None:
    """Writes the details of the holdings to the report."""
    ws_holdings_count: int = 1
    for ws_hold_idx in range(1, ws_holdings_count + 1):
        rpt_symbol: str = "DUMMY"
        rpt_shares: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")
        rpt_price: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")
        rpt_value: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")
        rpt_gain: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")
        ws_holdings_line: str = "DUMMY"
        write_report_record_C7(ws_holdings_line)

def write_report_record_C7(report_record: str) -> None:
    """Writes the report record (dummy)."""
    pass

def quarterly_report_C7() -> None:
    """Generates a quarterly performance report."""
    rpt_title: str = 'QUARTERLY PERFORMANCE REPORT'
    ws_total_value: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")
    ws_quarter_start_value: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")
    rpt_quarter_return: Decimal = (ws_total_value - ws_quarter_start_value) / ws_quarter_start_value * 100
    ws_performance_line: str = "DUMMY"
    write_report_record_C7(ws_performance_line)

def annual_tax_report_C7() -> None:
    """Generates an annual tax report."""
    rpt_title: str = 'ANNUAL TAX REPORT - 1099'
    ws_dividend_income: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")
    ws_realized_gain_ytd: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")
    rpt_dividends: Decimal = ws_dividend_income
    rpt_cap_gains: Decimal = ws_realized_gain_ytd
    ws_tax_line: str = "DUMMY"
    write_report_record_C7(ws_tax_line)

def trade_execution_C7() -> None:
    """Executes a trade."""
    validate_order_C7()
    ws_order_valid: str = 'Y'
    if ws_order_valid == 'Y':
        check_funds_shares_C7()
        ws_sufficient_flag: str = 'Y'
        if ws_sufficient_flag == 'Y':
            route_order_C7()
            execute_order_C7()
            settle_trade_C7()
        else:
            reject_order_C7()

def validate_order_C7() -> None:
    """Validates a trade order."""
    ws_order_valid: str = 'Y'
    ws_trade_symbol: str = " "
    ws_reject_reason: str = ""
    ws_trade_shares: int = 1
    order_limit: bool = False
    order_stop_limit: bool = False
    ws_limit_price: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")

    if ws_trade_symbol == " ":
        ws_order_valid = 'N'
        ws_reject_reason = 'SYMBOL REQUIRED'
        return
    if ws_trade_shares <= 0:
        ws_order_valid = 'N'
        ws_reject_reason = 'INVALID QUANTITY'
        return
    if order_limit or order_stop_limit:
        if ws_limit_price <= 0:
            ws_order_valid = 'N'
            ws_reject_reason = 'LIMIT PRICE REQUIRED'

def check_funds_shares_C7() -> None:
    """Checks for sufficient funds or shares."""
    trade_buy: bool = False
    trade_sell: bool = False
    ws_available_cash: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")
    ws_trade_shares: int = 1
    ws_estimated_price: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")

    ws_sufficient_flag: str = 'Y'
    ws_reject_reason: str = ""
    if trade_buy:
        ws_required_funds: Decimal = ws_trade_shares * ws_estimated_price
        if ws_required_funds > ws_available_cash:
            ws_sufficient_flag = 'N'
            ws_reject_reason = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
    if trade_sell:
        ws_current_shares: Decimal = check_share_position_C7()
        if ws_current_shares < ws_trade_shares:
            ws_sufficient_flag = 'N'
            ws_reject_reason = 'INSUFFICIENT SHARES'

def check_share_position_C7() -> Decimal:
    """Checks the current share position."""
    ws_current_shares: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_holdings_count: int = 1
    ws_trade_symbol: str = "DUMMY"

    for ws_hold_idx in range(1, ws_holdings_count + 1):
        hold_symbol: str = "DUMMY"
        hold_shares: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")
        if hold_symbol == ws_trade_symbol:
            ws_current_shares += hold_shares
    return ws_current_shares

def route_order_C7() -> None:
    """Routes the trade order."""
    ws_trade_amount: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")

    if ws_trade_amount > 100000:
        ws_routing_type: str = 'ALGO'
    elif ws_trade_amount > 10000:
        ws_routing_type: str = 'SMART'
    else:
        ws_routing_type: str = 'DIRECT'
    ws_order_time: int = 20240101

def execute_order_C7() -> None:
    """Executes the trade order."""
    order_market: bool = False
    order_limit: bool = False
    order_stop: bool = False

    if order_market:
        market_order_C7()
    elif order_limit:
        limit_order_C7()
    elif order_stop:
        stop_order_C7()
    else:
        stop_limit_order_C7()

def market_order_C7() -> None:
    """Executes a market order."""
    ws_current_market_price: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")

    ws_executed_price: Decimal = ws_current_market_price
    ws_trade_status: str = 'FILLED'
    ws_execution_time: int = 20240101

def limit_order_C7() -> None:
    """Executes a limit order."""
    trade_buy: bool = False
    ws_current_market_price: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")
    ws_limit_price: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")

    ws_executed_price: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_trade_status: str = ""

    if trade_buy:
        if ws_current_market_price <= ws_limit_price:
            ws_executed_price = ws_current_market_price
            ws_trade_status = 'FILLED'
        else:
            ws_trade_status = 'OPEN'
    else:
        if ws_current_market_price >= ws_limit_price:
            ws_executed_price = ws_current_market_price
            ws_trade_status = 'FILLED'
        else:
            ws_trade_status = 'OPEN'

def stop_order_C7() -> None:
    """Executes a stop order."""
    trade_sell: bool = False
    ws_current_market_price: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")
    ws_stop_price: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")

    ws_executed_price: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_trade_status: str = ""

    if trade_sell:
        if ws_current_market_price <= ws_stop_price:
            ws_executed_price = ws_current_market_price
            ws_trade_status = 'FILLED'
        else:
            ws_trade_status = 'OPEN'

def stop_limit_order_C7() -> None:
    """Executes a stop limit order."""
    ws_current_market_price: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")
    ws_stop_price: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")
    ws_trade_status: str = ""

    if ws_current_market_price <= ws_stop_price:
        limit_order_C7()
    else:
        ws_trade_status = 'OPEN'

def settle_trade_C7() -> None:
    """Settles the trade."""
    ws_trade_status: str = 'FILLED'

    if ws_trade_status == 'FILLED':
        calculate_costs_C7()
        update_positions_C7()
        update_cash_C7()
        record_trade_C7()

def calculate_costs_C7() -> None:
    """Calculates the costs associated with the trade."""
    ws_trade_shares: int = 1
    ws_executed_price: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")
    trade_buy: bool = False

    ws_gross_amount: Decimal = ws_trade_shares * ws_executed_price
    if ws_gross_amount > 100000:
        ws_commission: Decimal = ws_gross_amount * Decimal("0.0005")
    elif ws_gross_amount > 10000:
        ws_commission: Decimal = ws_gross_amount * Decimal("0.001")
    else:
        ws_commission = Decimal("4.95")
    ws_fees: Decimal = ws_gross_amount * Decimal("0.00002")
    if trade_buy:
        ws_net_amount: Decimal = ws_gross_amount + ws_commission + ws_fees
    else:
        ws_net_amount = ws_gross_amount - ws_commission - ws_fees

def update_positions_C7() -> None:
    """Updates the positions after the trade."""
    trade_buy: bool = False

    if trade_buy:
        add_to_position_C7()
    else:
        reduce_position_C7()

def add_to_position_C7() -> None:
    """Adds to an existing position."""
    ws_trade_symbol: str = "DUMMY"
    ws_trade_shares: int = 1
    ws_executed_price: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")
    ws_holdings_count: int = 1
    found: bool = False

    for ws_hold_idx in range(1, ws_holdings_count + 1):
        hold_symbol: str = "DUMMY"
        hold_shares: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")
        hold_cost_per_share: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")
        if hold_symbol == ws_trade_symbol:
            found = True
            ws_new_total_shares: Decimal = hold_shares + ws_trade_shares
            ws_new_cost: Decimal = (hold_shares * hold_cost_per_share) + (ws_trade_shares * ws_executed_price)
            hold_cost_per_share = ws_new_cost / ws_new_total_shares
            hold_shares = ws_new_total_shares
            break

    if not found:
        create_new_position_C7()

def reduce_position_C7() -> None:
    """Reduces an existing position."""
    ws_trade_symbol: str = "DUMMY"
    ws_trade_shares: int = 1
    ws_realized_gain_ytd: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_holdings_count: int = 1

    for ws_hold_idx in range(1, ws_holdings_count + 1):
        hold_symbol: str = "DUMMY"
        hold_shares: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")
        hold_cost_per_share: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")
        ws_executed_price: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")

        if hold_symbol == ws_trade_symbol:
            hold_shares -= ws_trade_shares
            ws_realized_gain: Decimal = ws_trade_shares * (ws_executed_price - hold_cost_per_share)
            ws_realized_gain_ytd += ws_realized_gain
            break

def create_new_position_C7() -> None:
    """Creates a new position in the portfolio."""
    ws_trade_symbol: str = "DUMMY"
    ws_trade_shares: int = 1
    ws_executed_price: Decimal = Decimal("1.00")

    ws_holdings_count: int = 1
    ws_holdings_count += 1

def

def calc_auto_premium_C8(ws_driver_age: int, ws_base_premium: Decimal, ws_accidents_3yr: int, ws_violations_3yr: int, ws_annual_premium: Decimal, ws_monthly_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates the auto insurance premium."""
    if 6 <= ws_driver_age <= 10:
        ws_base_premium += 100
    else:
        ws_base_premium += 50
    if ws_driver_age < 25:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
    if ws_accidents_3yr > 0:
        ws_accident_surcharge = ws_accidents_3yr * 200
        ws_base_premium += ws_accident_surcharge
    if ws_violations_3yr > 0:
        ws_violation_surcharge = ws_violations_3yr * 100
        ws_base_premium += ws_violation_surcharge
    ws_annual_premium = ws_base_premium
    ws_monthly_premium = ws_annual_premium / 12

def calc_home_premium_C8(ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_home_age: int, ws_flood_zone: str, ws_security_system: str, ws_deductible: int, ws_base_premium: Decimal, ws_annual_premium: Decimal, ws_monthly_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates the home insurance premium."""
    ws_base_premium = ws_coverage_amount * Decimal('0.003')
    if 0 <= ws_home_age <= 10:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.9')
    elif 11 <= ws_home_age <= 25:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.0')
    elif 26 <= ws_home_age <= 50:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.2')
    else:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
    if ws_flood_zone == 'Y':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
    if ws_security_system == 'Y':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.9')
    ws_deductible_credit = ws_deductible / 1000 * 50
    ws_base_premium -= ws_deductible_credit
    if ws_base_premium < 200:
        ws_base_premium = Decimal('200')
    ws_annual_premium = ws_base_premium
    ws_monthly_premium = ws_annual_premium / 12

def calc_health_premium_C8(ws_insured_age: int, ws_plan_type: str, ws_family_plan: str, ws_base_premium: Decimal, ws_monthly_premium: Decimal, ws_annual_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates the health insurance premium."""
    ws_base_premium = Decimal('300')
    if 0 <= ws_insured_age <= 18:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.5')
    elif 19 <= ws_insured_age <= 30:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.0')
    elif 31 <= ws_insured_age <= 40:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.3')
    elif 41 <= ws_insured_age <= 50:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.6')
    elif 51 <= ws_insured_age <= 60:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('2.0')
    else:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('2.8')
    if ws_plan_type == 'BRONZE':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.8')
    elif ws_plan_type == 'SILVER':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.0')
    elif ws_plan_type == 'GOLD':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.3')
    elif ws_plan_type == 'PLATINUM':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.6')
    if ws_family_plan == 'Y':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('2.5')
    ws_monthly_premium = ws_base_premium
    ws_annual_premium = ws_monthly_premium * 12

def underwriting_C8() -> None:
    """Performs underwriting tasks."""
    evaluate_risk_factors_C8()
    check_medical_history_C8()
    verify_information_C8()
    determine_decision_C8()

def evaluate_risk_factors_C8(policy_life: bool, ws_bmi: int, ws_smoker_flag: str, ws_hazardous_occupation: str, policy_auto: bool, ws_driver_age: int, ws_accidents_3yr: int, ws_risk_points: int) -> None:
    """Evaluates risk factors."""
    ws_risk_points = 0
    if policy_life:
        if ws_bmi > 30:
            ws_risk_points += 10
        if ws_smoker_flag == 'Y':
            ws_risk_points += 25
        if ws_hazardous_occupation == 'Y':
            ws_risk_points += 15
    if policy_auto:
        if ws_driver_age < 21:
            ws_risk_points += 20
        if ws_accidents_3yr > 1:
            ws_risk_points += 15

def check_medical_history_C8(ws_chronic_conditions: int, ws_recent_hospitalization: str, ws_prescription_count: int, ws_risk_points: int) -> None:
    """Checks medical history."""
    if ws_chronic_conditions > 0:
        ws_condition_points = ws_chronic_conditions * 5
        ws_risk_points += ws_condition_points
    if ws_recent_hospitalization == 'Y':
        ws_risk_points += 10
    if ws_prescription_count > 5:
        ws_risk_points += 5

def verify_information_C8() -> None:
    """Verifies information."""
    check_fraud_indicators_C8()
    validate_documents_C8()

def check_fraud_indicators_C8(ws_recent_claims: int, ws_address_mismatch: str, ws_risk_points: int, ws_fraud_flag: str) -> None:
    """Checks for fraud indicators."""
    if ws_recent_claims > 3:
        ws_risk_points += 20
        ws_fraud_flag = 'Y'
    if ws_address_mismatch == 'Y':
        ws_risk_points += 10

def validate_documents_C8(ws_doc_missing: str, ws_uw_status: str) -> None:
    """Validates documents."""
    if ws_doc_missing == 'Y':
        ws_uw_status = 'PENDING'
    else:
        ws_uw_status = 'COMPLETE'

def determine_decision_C8(ws_risk_points: int, ws_uw_decision: str, ws_annual_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Determines the underwriting decision."""
    if ws_risk_points > 50:
        ws_uw_decision = 'DECLINE'
    elif ws_risk_points > 30:
        ws_uw_decision = 'SUBSTANDARD'
        ws_annual_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
    elif ws_risk_points > 15:
        ws_uw_decision = 'STANDARD'
    else:
        ws_uw_decision = 'PREFERRED'
        ws_annual_premium *= Decimal('0.9')

def issue_policy_C8(ws_uw_decision: str) -> None:
    """Issues the policy."""
    if ws_uw_decision != 'DECLINE':
        generate_policy_number_C8()
        create_policy_record_C8()
        set_beneficiaries_C8()
        send_policy_docs_C8()
    else:
        send_decline_letter_C8()

def generate_policy_number_C8(ws_date_part: str, ws_policy_type: str, ws_type_part: str, ws_random_part: int, ws_policy_number: str) -> None:
    """Generates the policy number."""
    ws_date_part = "current_date()"
    ws_type_part = ws_policy_type
    ws_random_part = int(random_C8() * 99999)
    ws_policy_number = ws_type_part + ws_date_part + str(ws_random_part)

def create_policy_record_C8(ws_policy_number: str, ws_policy_type: str, ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_annual_premium: Decimal, ws_effective_date: str, ws_expiration_date: str, ws_policy_record: Any) -> None:
    """Creates the policy record."""
    ws_policy_record = {}
    policy_rec_number = ws_policy_number
    policy_rec_type = ws_policy_type
    policy_rec_coverage = ws_coverage_amount
    policy_rec_premium = ws_annual_premium
    policy_rec_eff_date = ws_effective_date
    policy_rec_exp_date = ws_expiration_date
    policy_rec_status = 'A'
    write_policy_record_C8(ws_policy_record)

def set_beneficiaries_C8(ws_policy_number: str, ws_beneficiary_rec: Any, benef_name: list[str], benef_relation: list[str], benef_pct: list[Decimal]) -> None:
    """Sets the beneficiaries."""
    for ws_benef_idx in range(1, 6):
        if benef_name[ws_benef_idx - 1].strip() != "":
            ws_beneficiary_rec = {}
            benef_rec_policy = ws_policy_number
            benef_rec_name = benef_name[ws_benef_idx - 1]
            benef_rec_relation = benef_relation[ws_benef_idx - 1]
            benef_rec_pct = benef_pct[ws_benef_idx - 1]
            write_beneficiary_record_C8(ws_beneficiary_rec)

def send_policy_docs_C8(ws_policy_number: str) -> None:
    """Sends the policy documents."""
    ws_notif_type = 'policy_issue'
    ws_notif_channel = 'MAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = 'Your policy ' + ws_policy_number + ' has been issued'
    send_notification_C8()

def send_decline_letter_C8() -> None:
    """Sends the decline letter."""
    ws_notif_type = 'policy_decline'
    ws_notif_channel = 'MAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = 'Regarding your insurance application'
    send_notification_C8()

def claims_handling_C8() -> None:
    """Handles claims."""
    receive_claim_C8()
    validate_claim_C8()
    investigate_claim_C8()
    adjudicate_claim_C8()
    process_payment_C8()

def receive_claim_C8(ws_claim_date: str, ws_claim_status: str) -> None:
    """Receives a claim."""
    ws_claim_date = "current_date()"
    generate_claim_number_C8()
    ws_claim_status = 'RECEIVED'

def generate_claim_number_C8(ws_date_part: str, ws_random_part: int, ws_claim_number: str) -> None:
    """Generates a claim number."""
    ws_date_part = "current_date()"
    ws_random_part = int(random_C8() * 99999)
    ws_claim_number = 'CLM' + ws_date_part + str(ws_random_part)

def validate_claim_C8() -> None:
    """Validates a claim."""
    check_policy_status_C8()
    check_coverage_C8()
    check_deductible_C8()

def check_policy_status_C8(ws_policy_status: str, ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_deny_reason: str) -> None:
    """Checks the policy status."""
    if ws_policy_status != 'A':
        ws_claim_status = 'DENIED'
        ws_claim_deny_reason = 'POLICY NOT ACTIVE'

def check_coverage_C8(ws_claim_type: str, ws_covered_perils: str, ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_deny_reason: str) -> None:
    """Checks the coverage."""
    if ws_claim_type != ws_covered_perils:
        ws_claim_status = 'DENIED'
        ws_claim_deny_reason = 'NOT COVERED PERIL'

def check_deductible_C8(ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_deductible: int, ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_deny_reason: str) -> None:
    """Checks the deductible."""
    if ws_claim_amount <= ws_deductible:
        ws_claim_status = 'DENIED'
        ws_claim_deny_reason = 'BELOW DEDUCTIBLE'

def investigate_claim_C8(ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_claim_status: str, ws_recent_claims: int, ws_fraud_review: str) -> None:
    """Investigates a claim."""
    if ws_claim_amount > 10000:
        ws_claim_status = 'INVESTIGATION'
        assign_adjuster_C8()
    fraud_check_C8(ws_recent_claims, ws_claim_amount, ws_coverage_amount, ws_fraud_review)

def assign_adjuster_C8(ws_adjuster_id: str, ws_notes: str) -> None:
    """Assigns an adjuster."""
    ws_adjuster_id = 'ADJ001'
    ws_notes = 'Assigned for investigation'

def fraud_check_C8(ws_recent_claims: int, ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_fraud_review: str) -> None:
    """Checks for fraud."""
    if ws_recent_claims > 2:
        ws_fraud_review = 'Y'
    if ws_claim_amount > ws_coverage_amount * Decimal('0.8'):
        ws_fraud_review = 'Y'

def adjudicate_claim_C8(ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_deductible: int, ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_approved_amount: Decimal) -> None:
    """Adjudicates a claim."""
    if ws_claim_status != 'DENIED':
        ws_approved_amount = ws_claim_amount - ws_deductible
        if ws_approved_amount > ws_coverage_amount:
            ws_approved_amount = ws_coverage_amount
        ws_claim_status = 'APPROVED'

def process_payment_C8(ws_claim_status: str) -> None:
    """Processes a payment."""
    if ws_claim_status == 'APPROVED':
        issue_payment_C8()
        update_claim_record_C8()

def issue_payment_C8(ws_claim_number: str, ws_approved_amount: Decimal, ws_payment_record: Any, pay_rec_claim: str, pay_rec_amount: Decimal, pay_rec_date: str, pay_rec_method: str) -> None:
    """Issues a payment."""
    ws_payment_record = {}
    pay_rec_claim = ws_claim_number
    pay_rec_amount = ws_approved_amount
    pay_rec_date = "current_date()"
    pay_rec_method = 'CHECK'
    write_payment_record_C8(ws_payment_record)

def update_claim_record_C8(ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_close_date: str) -> None:
    """Updates the claim record."""
    ws_claim_status = 'PAID'
    ws_claim_close_date = "current_date()"
    rewrite_claim_record_C8()

def payroll_processing_C8() -> None:
    """Processes payroll."""
    load_employee_data_C8()
    calculate_gross_pay_C8()
    calculate_taxes_C8()
    calculate_deductions_C8()
    calculate_net_pay_C8()
    generate_paystubs_C8()
    process_direct_deposit_C8()

def load_employee_data_C8(ws_employee_id: str, emp_search_key: str, ws_employee_rec: Any) -> None:
    """Loads employee data."""
    emp_search_key = ws_employee_id
    try:
        ws_employee_rec = read_employee_file_C8(emp_search_key)
    except Exception:
        ws_error_msg = 'EMPLOYEE NOT FOUND'
        handle_error_C8()

def calculate_gross_pay_C8(ws_pay_type: str) -> None:
    """Calculates gross pay."""
    if ws_pay_type == 'SALARY':
        calc_salary_pay_C8()
    elif ws_pay_type == 'HOURLY':
        calc_hourly_pay_C8()
    elif ws_pay_type == 'COMMISSION':
        calc_commission_pay_C8()

def calc_salary_pay_C8(ws_annual_salary: Decimal, ws_pay_periods: int, ws_gross_pay: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates salary pay."""
    ws_gross_pay = ws_annual_salary / ws_pay_periods

def calc_hourly_pay_C8(ws_hours_worked: Decimal, ws_hourly_rate: Decimal, ws_regular_pay: Decimal, ws_overtime_pay: Decimal, ws_ot_hours: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates hourly pay."""
    if ws_hours_worked <= 40:
        ws_regular_pay = ws_hours_worked * ws_hourly_rate
        ws_overtime_pay = Decimal('0')
    else:
        ws_regular_pay = 40 * ws_hourly_rate
        ws_ot_hours = ws_hours_worked - 40
        ws_overtime_pay = ws_ot_hours * ws_hourly_rate * Decimal('1.5')
    ws_gross_pay = ws_regular_pay + ws_overtime_pay

def calc_commission_pay_C8(ws_base_salary: Decimal, ws_pay_periods: int, ws_sales_amount: Decimal, ws_commission_rate: Decimal, ws_base_pay: Decimal, ws_commission_pay: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates commission pay."""
    ws_base_pay = ws_base_salary / ws_pay_periods
    ws_commission_pay = ws_sales_amount * ws_commission_rate
    ws_gross_pay = ws_base_pay + ws_commission_pay

def calculate_taxes_C8() -> None:
    """Calculates taxes."""
    calc_federal_tax_C8()
    calc_state_tax_C8()
    calc_local_tax_C8()
    calc_fica_C8()

def calc_federal_tax_C8(ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_pay_periods: int, ws_exemptions: int) -> None:
    """Calculates federal tax."""
    ws_annualized_gross = ws_gross_pay * ws_pay_periods
    ws_allowance_amount = ws_exemptions * 4300
    ws_taxable_income = ws_annualized_gross - ws_allowance_amount
    if ws_taxable_income < 0:
        ws_taxable_income = Decimal('0')
    apply_tax_brackets_C8(ws_taxable_income)
    ws_federal_tax = ws_annual_tax / ws_pay_periods

def apply_tax_brackets_C8(ws_taxable_income: Decimal) -> None:
    """Applies tax brackets."""
    ws_annual_tax = Decimal('0')
    if status_single_C8():
        single_brackets_C8(ws_taxable_income)
    elif status_married_joint_C8():
        married_brackets_C8(ws_taxable_income)

def single_brackets_C8(ws_taxable_income: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates tax for single filers."""
    if ws_taxable_income <= 10275:
        ws_annual_tax = ws_taxable_income * Decimal('0.10')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 41775:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('1027.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 10275) * Decimal('0.12')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 89075:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('4807.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 41775) * Decimal('0.22')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 170050:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('15213.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 89075) * Decimal('0.24')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 215950:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('34647.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 170050) * Decimal('0.32')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 539900:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('49335.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 215950) * Decimal('0.35')
    else:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('162718.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 539900) * Decimal('0.37')

def married_brackets_C8(ws_taxable_income: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates tax for married filers."""
    if ws_taxable_income <= 20550:
        ws_annual_tax = ws_taxable_income * Decimal('0.10')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 83550:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('2055.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 20550) * Decimal('0.12')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 178150:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('9615.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 83550) * Decimal('0.22')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 340100:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('30427.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 178150) * Decimal('0.24')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 431900:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('69295.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 340100) * Decimal('0.32')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 647850:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('98671.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 431900) * Decimal('0.35')
    else:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('174253.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 647850) * Decimal('0.37')

def calc_state_tax_C8(ws_state_code: str, ws_gross_pay: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates state tax."""
    if ws_state_code == 'CA':
        ws_state_tax = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.0725')
    elif ws_state_code == 'NY':
        ws_state_tax = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.0685')
    elif ws_state_code == 'TX':
        ws_state_tax = Decimal('0')
    elif ws_state_code == 'FL':
        ws_state_tax = Decimal('0')
    else:
        ws_state_tax = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.05')

def calc_local_tax_C8(ws_local_tax_rate: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates local tax."""
    if ws_local_tax_rate > 0:
        ws_local_tax = ws_gross_pay * ws_local_tax_rate
    else:
        ws_local_tax = Decimal('0')

def calc_fica_C8(ws_ytd_gross: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates FICA taxes."""
    if ws_ytd_gross < 160200:
        ws_remaining_cap = 160200 - ws_ytd_gross
        if ws_gross_pay <= ws_remaining_cap:
            ws_fica_ss = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.062')
        else:
            ws_fica_ss = ws_remaining_cap * Decimal('0.062')
    else:
        ws_fica_ss = Decimal('0')
    ws_fica_medicare = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.0145')
    if ws_ytd_gross > 200000:
        ws_additional_medicare = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.009')
        ws_fica_medicare += ws_additional_medicare

def calculate_deductions_C8() -> None:
    """Calculates deductions."""
    calc_pre_tax_deductions_C8()
    calc_post_tax_deductions_C8()

def calc_pre_tax_deductions_C8(ws_401k_pct: Decimal, ws_ytd_401k: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_health_ins_deduct: Decimal, ws_dental_ins_deduct: Decimal, ws_vision_ins_deduct: Decimal, ws_hsa_deduct: Decimal, ws_fsa_deduct: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates pre-tax deductions."""
    if ws_401k_pct > 0:
        ws_401k_contrib = ws_gross_pay * ws_401k_pct / 100
        if ws_ytd_401k + ws_401k_contrib > 22500:
            ws_401k_contrib = 22500 - ws_ytd_401k
            if ws_401k_contrib < 0:
                ws_401k_contrib = Decimal('0')
    else:
        ws_401k_contrib = Decimal('0')
    ws_health_ins = ws_health_ins_deduct
    ws_dental_ins = ws_dental_ins_deduct
    ws_vision_ins = ws_vision_ins_deduct
    ws_hsa_contrib = ws_hsa_deduct
    ws_fsa_contrib = ws_fsa_deduct

def calc_post_tax_deductions_C8(ws_life_ins_deduct: Decimal, ws_disability_deduct: Decimal, ws_union_dues_amt: Decimal, ws_garnishment_amt: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates post-tax deductions."""
    ws_life_ins = ws_life_ins_deduct
    ws_disability_ins = ws_disability_deduct
    ws_union_dues = ws_union_dues_amt
    ws_garnishment = ws_garnishment_amt
    ws_other_deduct = Decimal('0')

def calculate_net_pay_C8(ws_federal_tax: Decimal, ws_state_tax: Decimal, ws_local_tax: Decimal, ws_fica_ss: Decimal, ws_fica_medicare: Decimal, ws_health_ins: Decimal, ws_dental_ins: Decimal, ws_vision_ins: Decimal, ws_401k_contrib: Decimal, ws_hsa_contrib: Decimal, ws_fsa_contrib: Decimal, ws_life_ins: Decimal, ws_disability_ins: Decimal, ws_union_dues: Decimal, ws_garnishment: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates net pay."""
    ws_total_deductions = (ws_federal_tax + ws_state_tax + ws_local_tax + 0  # TODO
                           ws_fica_ss + ws_fica_medicare + 0  # TODO
                           ws_health_ins + ws_dental_ins + ws_vision_ins + 0  # TODO
                           ws_401k_contrib + ws_hsa_contrib + ws_fsa_contrib + 0  # TODO
                           ws_life_ins + ws_disability_ins + 0  # TODO
                           ws_union_dues + ws_garnishment + ws_other_deduct)
    ws_net_pay = ws_gross_pay - ws_total_deductions
    update_ytd_totals_C8(ws_gross_pay, ws_federal_tax, ws_state_tax, ws_fica_ss, ws_fica_medicare, ws_net_pay, ws_401k_contrib)

def update_ytd_totals_C8(ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_federal_tax: Decimal, ws_state_tax: Decimal, ws_fica_ss: Decimal, ws_fica_medicare: Decimal, ws_net_pay: Decimal, ws_401k_contrib: Decimal) -> None:
    """Updates year-to-date totals."""
    ws_ytd_gross += ws_gross_pay
    ws_ytd_fed_tax += ws_federal_tax
    ws_ytd_state_tax += ws_state_tax
    ws_ytd_fica += ws_fica_ss
    ws_ytd_fica += ws_fica_medicare
    ws_ytd_net += ws_net_pay
    ws_ytd_401k += ws_401k_contrib

def generate_paystubs_C8(ws_employee_id: str, ws_pay_period: str, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_federal_tax: Decimal, ws_state_tax: Decimal, ws_fica_ss: Decimal, ws_fica_medicare: Decimal, ws_net_pay: Decimal, ws_ytd_gross: Decimal, ws_ytd_net: Decimal, ws_paystub_record: Any) -> None:
    """Generates paystubs."""
    ws_paystub_record = {}
    stub_emp_id = ws_employee_id
    stub_pay_period = ws_pay_period
    stub_gross = ws_gross_pay
    stub_fed_tax = ws_federal_tax
    stub_state_tax = ws_state_tax
    stub_ss = ws_fica_ss
    stub_medicare = ws_fica_medicare
    stub_net = ws_net_pay
    stub_ytd_gross = ws_ytd_gross
    stub_ytd_net = ws_ytd_net
    write_paystub_record_C8(ws_paystub_record)

def process_direct_deposit_C8(ws_dd_enabled: str) -> None:
    """Processes direct deposit."""
    if ws_dd_enabled == 'Y':
        validate_bank_info_C8()
        create_ach_record_C8()

def validate_bank_info_C8(ws_routing_number: str, ws_account_number: str) -> None:
    """Validates bank information."""
    if ws_routing_number.strip() == "":
        ws_dd_valid = 'N'
    elif ws_account_number.strip() == "":
        ws_dd_valid = 'N'
    else:
        ws_dd_valid = 'Y'

def create_ach_record_C8(ws_dd_valid: str, ws_routing_number: str, ws_account_number: str, ws_net_pay: Decimal, ws_pay_date: str, ws_ach_record: Any) -> None:
    """Creates an ACH record."""
    if ws_dd_valid == 'Y':
        ws_ach_record = {}
        ach_routing = ws_routing_number
        ach_account = ws_account_number
        ach_amount = ws_net_pay
        ach_date = ws_pay_date
        ach_desc = 'PAYROLL'
        write_ach_record_C8(ws_ach_record)

    elif ws_notif_channel == 'PUSH':

        pass

def calculate_next_run_date_C10(ws_last_run_date: Any, ws_next_run_date: Any, schedule_type: str) -> None:
    """Calculates the next run date based on schedule type."""
    if schedule_type == 'DAILY':
        ws_next_run_date = integer_of_date_C10(ws_last_run_date) + 1
    elif schedule_type == 'WEEKLY':
        ws_next_run_date = integer_of_date_C10(ws_last_run_date) + 7
    elif schedule_type == 'MONTHLY':
        ws_next_run_date = integer_of_date_C10(ws_last_run_date) + 30
    elif schedule_type == 'QUARTERLY':
        ws_next_run_date = integer_of_date_C10(ws_last_run_date) + 90
    elif schedule_type == 'YEARLY':
        ws_next_run_date = integer_of_date_C10(ws_last_run_date) + 365
    else:
        pass

def integer_of_date_C10(date: Any) -> int:
    """Dummy function for integer of date."""
    return 0

    collect_customer_metrics_C10()
    collect_performance_metrics_C10()

def collect_transaction_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """Collects transaction metrics."""
    global ws_total_trans_amount_C10, ws_total_trans_count_C10, ws_avg_trans_amount_C10, ws_eof_flag_C10
    ws_total_trans_amount_C10 = 0
    ws_total_trans_count_C10 = 0
    ws_avg_trans_amount_C10 = 0
    ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C10 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_trans_rec_C10 = read_transaction_file_C10()
            ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'
            ws_total_trans_count_C10 += 1
            ws_total_trans_amount_C10 += ws_trans_rec_C10.trans_amount
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'Y'
    if ws_total_trans_count_C10 > 0:
        ws_avg_trans_amount_C10 = ws_total_trans_amount_C10 / ws_total_trans_count_C10
    ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'

@dataclass
class WsTransRecC10:

def collect_customer_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """Collects customer metrics."""
    global ws_active_customers_C10, ws_new_customers_C10, ws_churned_customers_C10, ws_eof_flag_C10
    ws_active_customers_C10 = 0
    ws_new_customers_C10 = 0
    ws_churned_customers_C10 = 0
    ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C10 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_cust_rec_C10 = read_customer_file_C10()
            ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'
            if ws_cust_rec_C10.cust_status == 'A':
                ws_active_customers_C10 += 1
            if ws_cust_rec_C10.cust_open_date >= ws_period_start_C10:
                ws_new_customers_C10 += 1
            if ws_cust_rec_C10.cust_close_date >= ws_period_start_C10:
                ws_churned_customers_C10 += 1
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'

    cust_status: str
    cust_open_date: Any
    cust_close_date: Any

def read_customer_file_C10() -> WsCustRecC10:
    """Dummy function to read a customer file."""
    raise EOFError

def collect_performance_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """Collects performance metrics."""
    global ws_response_time_total_C10, ws_response_count_C10, ws_avg_response_time_C10, ws_eof_flag_C10
    ws_response_time_total_C10 = 0
    ws_response_count_C10 = 0
    ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C10 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_perf_rec_C10 = read_perf_log_file_C10()
            ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'
            ws_response_time_total_C10 += ws_perf_rec_C10.perf_response_time
            ws_response_count_C10 += 1
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'Y'
    if ws_response_count_C10 > 0:
        ws_avg_response_time_C10 = ws_response_time_total_C10 / ws_response_count_C10
    ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'

def aggregate_data_C10() -> None:
    """Aggregates data."""
    daily_aggregation_C10()
    weekly_aggregation_C10()
    monthly_aggregation_C10()

def daily_aggregation_C10() -> None:
    """Performs daily aggregation."""
    global ws_daily_summary_C10, ws_process_date_C10, ws_total_trans_count_C10, ws_total_trans_amount_C10, ws_total_deposits_C10, ws_total_withdrawals_C10
    ws_daily_summary_C10 = WsDailySummaryC10()
    ws_daily_summary_C10.daily_date = ws_process_date_C10
    ws_daily_summary_C10.daily_trans_count = ws_total_trans_count_C10
    ws_daily_summary_C10.daily_trans_amount = ws_total_trans_amount_C10
    ws_daily_summary_C10.daily_deposits = ws_total_deposits_C10
    ws_daily_summary_C10.daily_withdrawals = ws_total_withdrawals_C10
    write_daily_summary_record_C10(ws_daily_summary_C10)

def weekly_aggregation_C10() -> None:
    """Performs weekly aggregation."""
    global ws_day_of_week_C10, ws_weekly_summary_C10, ws_week_number_C10
    if ws_day_of_week_C10 == 7:
        ws_weekly_summary_C10 = WsWeeklySummaryC10()
        ws_weekly_summary_C10.weekly_week = ws_week_number_C10
        sum_week_data_C10()
        write_weekly_summary_record_C10(ws_weekly_summary_C10)

    """Dummy function to write a weekly summary record."""
    pass

def sum_week_data_C10() -> None:
    """Sums weekly data."""
    global weekly_trans_count_C10, weekly_trans_amount_C10, daily_trans_count_C10, daily_trans_amount_C10
    weekly_trans_count_C10 = 0
    weekly_trans_amount_C10 = 0
    for _ in range(7):
        weekly_trans_count_C10 += daily_trans_count_C10
        weekly_trans_amount_C10 += daily_trans_amount_C10

def monthly_aggregation_C10() -> None:
    """Performs monthly aggregation."""
    global ws_end_of_month_C10, ws_monthly_summary_C10, ws_curr_month_C10, ws_curr_year_C10
    if ws_end_of_month_C10 == 'Y':
        ws_monthly_summary_C10 = WsMonthlySummaryC10()
        ws_monthly_summary_C10.monthly_month = ws_curr_month_C10
        ws_monthly_summary_C10.monthly_year = ws_curr_year_C10
        sum_month_data_C10()
        write_monthly_summary_record_C10(ws_monthly_summary_C10)
