def sum_month_data_C10() -> None:
    """Sums monthly data."""
    global monthly_trans_count_C10, monthly_trans_amount_C10, monthly_new_accounts_C10, monthly_closed_accounts_C10, ws_eof_flag_C10, ws_curr_month_C10
    monthly_trans_count_C10 = 0
    monthly_trans_amount_C10 = 0
    monthly_new_accounts_C10 = 0
    monthly_closed_accounts_C10 = 0
    ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C10 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec_C10 = read_daily_summary_file_C10()
            ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'
            if ws_daily_sum_rec_C10.daily_month == ws_curr_month_C10:
                monthly_trans_count_C10 += ws_daily_sum_rec_C10.daily_trans_count
                monthly_trans_amount_C10 += ws_daily_sum_rec_C10.daily_trans_amount
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'

def calculate_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """Calculates KPIs."""
    calc_financial_kpi_C10()
    calc_operational_kpi_C10()
    calc_customer_kpi_C10()

def calc_financial_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """Calculates financial KPIs."""
    global ws_total_assets_C10, ws_net_income_C10, ws_roa_C10, ws_total_equity_C10, ws_roe_C10, ws_interest_expense_C10, ws_interest_income_C10, ws_earning_assets_C10, ws_nim_C10
    if ws_total_assets_C10 > 0:
        ws_roa_C10 = (ws_net_income_C10 / ws_total_assets_C10) * 100
    if ws_total_equity_C10 > 0:
        ws_roe_C10 = (ws_net_income_C10 / ws_total_equity_C10) * 100
    if ws_interest_expense_C10 > 0:
        ws_nim_C10 = ((ws_interest_income_C10 - ws_interest_expense_C10) / ws_earning_assets_C10) * 100

def calc_operational_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """Calculates operational KPIs."""
    global ws_total_trans_count_C10, ws_error_count_C10, ws_error_rate_C10, ws_within_sla_count_C10, ws_total_cases_C10, ws_sla_compliance_C10, ws_fcr_count_C10, ws_total_calls_C10, ws_first_call_resolution_C10
    if ws_total_trans_count_C10 > 0:
        ws_error_rate_C10 = (ws_error_count_C10 / ws_total_trans_count_C10) * 100
    ws_sla_compliance_C10 = (ws_within_sla_count_C10 / ws_total_cases_C10) * 100
    ws_first_call_resolution_C10 = (ws_fcr_count_C10 / ws_total_calls_C10) * 100

def calc_customer_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """Calculates customer KPIs."""
    global ws_active_customers_C10, ws_churned_customers_C10, ws_churn_rate_C10, ws_marketing_spend_C10, ws_new_customers_C10, ws_acquisition_cost_C10, ws_avg_revenue_per_customer_C10, ws_avg_customer_tenure_C10, ws_lifetime_value_C10
    if ws_active_customers_C10 > 0:
        ws_churn_rate_C10 = (ws_churned_customers_C10 / ws_active_customers_C10) * 100
    ws_acquisition_cost_C10 = ws_marketing_spend_C10 / ws_new_customers_C10
    ws_lifetime_value_C10 = ws_avg_revenue_per_customer_C10 * ws_avg_customer_tenure_C10

    global dash_title_C10, dash_revenue_C10, dash_net_income_C10, dash_roa_C10, dash_roe_C10, dash_customers_C10, ws_exec_dashboard_C10
    dash_title_C10 = 'EXECUTIVE DASHBOARD'
    dash_revenue_C10 = ws_total_revenue_C10

def export_csv_C10() -> None:
    """Exports data to CSV."""
    global ws_eof_flag_C10, ws_csv_header_C10, ws_csv_line_C10
    open_output_csv_export_file_C10()
    ws_csv_header_C10 = 'Date,TransCount,TransAmount,Deposits,Withdrawals'
    write_csv_record_C10(ws_csv_header_C10)
    ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C10 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec_C10 = read_daily_summary_file_C10()
            ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'
            ws_csv_line_C10 = f"{ws_daily_sum_rec_C10.daily_date},{ws_daily_sum_rec_C10.daily_trans_count},{ws_daily_sum_rec_C10.daily_trans_amount},{ws_daily_sum_rec_C10.daily_deposits},{ws_daily_sum_rec_C10.daily_withdrawals}"
            write_csv_record_C10(ws_csv_line_C10)
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'Y'
    close_csv_export_file_C10()
    ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'

    """Formats a JSON record."""
    global ws_json_line_C10, ws_json_comma_C10, ws_first_record_C10, ws_daily_sum_rec_C10
    if ws_first_record_C10 == 'Y':
        ws_json_comma_C10 = ','
    else:
        ws_json_comma_C10 = ''
        ws_first_record_C10 = 'Y'
    ws_json_line_C10 = f'{ws_json_comma_C10}{{"date":"{ws_daily_sum_rec_C10.daily_date}","transCount":{ws_daily_sum_rec_C10.daily_trans_count},"transAmount":{ws_daily_sum_rec_C10.daily_trans_amount}}}'
    write_json_record_C10(ws_json_line_C10)

def dormant_account_check_C10() -> None:
    """Checks for dormant accounts."""
    global ws_eof_flag_C10
    ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C10 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_account_rec_C10 = read_account_file_C10()
            ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'
            check_activity_C10(ws_account_rec_C10)
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'

def check_activity_C10(ws_account_rec_C10: WsAccountRecC10) -> None:
    """Checks account activity."""
    global ws_days_inactive_C10, ws_process_date_C10
    ws_days_inactive_C10 = integer_of_date_C10(ws_process_date_C10) - integer_of_date_C10(ws_account_rec_C10.acct_last_activity)
    if ws_days_inactive_C10 > 365:
        ws_account_rec_C10.acct_status = 'D'
        mark_dormant_C10(ws_account_rec_C10)

def mark_dormant_C10(ws_account_rec_C10: WsAccountRecC10) -> None:
    """Marks an account as dormant."""
    global ws_process_date_C10
    ws_account_rec_C10.acct_status_desc = 'DORMANT'
    ws_account_rec_C10.acct_dormant_date = ws_process_date_C10
    rewrite_account_record_C10(ws_account_rec_C10)
    send_dormant_notice_C10()

    """Dummy function to rewrite an account record."""
    pass

    ws_notif_type_C10 = 'dormant_notice'
    ws_notif_channel_C10 = 'MAIL'
    ws_notif_subject_C10 = 'Important: Your account is dormant'
    send_notification_C10()

    """Dummy function to send a notification."""
    pass

def escheatment_processing_C10() -> None:
    """Processes escheatment."""
    global ws_eof_flag_C10
    ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C10 != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_account_rec_C10 = read_account_file_C10()
            ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'
            if ws_account_rec_C10.acct_status == 'D':
                check_escheatment_C10(ws_account_rec_C10)
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag_C10 = 'N'

def card_blocking_C11() -> None:
    """Card blocking."""
    global CARD_STATUS, WS_BLOCK_REASON, CARD_BLOCK_REASON, WS_PROCESS_DATE, CARD_BLOCK_DATE, WS_CARD_RECORD, WS_NOTIF_TYPE, WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, WS_NOTIF_BODY
    CARD_STATUS = 'B'
    CARD_BLOCK_REASON  = None  # TODO: was WS_BLOCK_REASON
    CARD_BLOCK_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    # rewrite CARD_RECORD from WS_CARD_RECORD
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'card_blocked'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'SMS'
    WS_NOTIF_BODY = f'Your card has been blocked: {WS_BLOCK_REASON}'
    send_notification_C11()

def wire_transfer_C11() -> None:
    """Wire transfer."""
    validate_wire_request_C11()
    if WS_WIRE_VALID == 'Y':
        ofac_screening_C11()
        if WS_OFAC_CLEAR == 'Y':
            process_wire_C11()
            send_confirmation_C11()
        else:
            reject_wire_C11()

def validate_wire_request_C11() -> None:
    """Validate wire request."""
    global WS_WIRE_VALID, WS_WIRE_AMOUNT, WS_WIRE_REJECT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT, WS_CTR_REQUIRED
    WS_WIRE_VALID = 'Y'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT <= 0:
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
    if WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT == " ":
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > 10000:
        WS_CTR_REQUIRED = 'Y'

def ofac_screening_C11() -> None:
    """OFAC screening."""
    global WS_OFAC_CLEAR, WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME, OFAC_SEARCH_NAME, OFAC_MATCH_FOUND, OFAC_MATCH_SCORE, WS_WIRE_REJECT, WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK, OFAC_SEARCH_BANK
    WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'Y'
    OFAC_SEARCH_NAME  = None  # TODO: was WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME
    # call OFACSRCH using OFAC_REQUEST OFAC_RESPONSE
    if OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y':
        if OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85:
            WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N'
            WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'OFAC MATCH'
    OFAC_SEARCH_BANK  = None  # TODO: was WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK
    # call OFACSRCH using OFAC_REQUEST OFAC_RESPONSE
    if OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y':
        if OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85:
            WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N'
            WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BANK OFAC MATCH'

def process_wire_C11() -> None:
    """Process wire."""
    debit_originator_C11()
    create_wire_message_C11()
    transmit_wire_C11()
    record_wire_C11()

def debit_originator_C11() -> None:
    """Debit originator."""
    global WS_WIRE_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_WIRE_FEE
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_FEE
    update_account_C11()

def create_wire_message_C11() -> None:
    """Create wire message."""
    global WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE, SWIFT_MSG_TYPE, WS_WIRE_REF, WS_WIRE_DATE, WS_WIRE_CURRENCY, WS_WIRE_AMOUNT, WS_ORIGINATOR_NAME, WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT, WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME, WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT, WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK_BIC, WS_PURPOSE
    # initialize WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE
    SWIFT_MSG_TYPE = 'MT103'
    WS_WIRE_REF  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_REF
    WS_WIRE_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_DATE
    WS_WIRE_CURRENCY  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_CURRENCY
    WS_WIRE_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
    WS_ORIGINATOR_NAME  = None  # TODO: was WS_ORIGINATOR_NAME
    WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT = WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT
    WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME  = None  # TODO: was WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME
    WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT = WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT
    WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK_BIC = WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK_BIC
    WS_PURPOSE  = None  # TODO: was WS_PURPOSE

def transmit_wire_C11() -> None:
    """Transmit wire."""
    global WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE, WS_SWIFT_RESPONSE, SWIFT_STATUS, WS_WIRE_STATUS
    # call SWIFTSEND using WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE WS_SWIFT_RESPONSE
    if SWIFT_STATUS == 'ACK':
        WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'SENT'
    else:
        WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'FAILED'
        reverse_debit_C11()

def record_wire_C11() -> None:
    """Record wire."""
    global WS_WIRE_RECORD, WIRE_REF, WS_WIRE_AMOUNT, WIRE_AMOUNT, WS_WIRE_STATUS, WIRE_STATUS, WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT, WIRE_FROM_ACCT, WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT, WIRE_TO_ACCT, WS_PROCESS_DATE, WIRE_DATE
    # initialize WS_WIRE_RECORD
    WIRE_REF  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_REF
    WIRE_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
    WIRE_STATUS  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_STATUS
    WIRE_FROM_ACCT = WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT
    WIRE_TO_ACCT = WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT
    WIRE_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    # write WIRE_RECORD from WS_WIRE_RECORD

def reverse_debit_C11() -> None:
    """Reverse debit."""
    global WS_WIRE_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_WIRE_FEE
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_FEE
    update_account_C11()

def send_confirmation_C11() -> None:
    """Send confirmation."""
    global WS_NOTIF_TYPE, WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT, WS_WIRE_REF
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'wire_confirm'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
    WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = f'Wire transfer {WS_WIRE_REF} completed'
    send_notification_C11()

def reject_wire_C11() -> None:
    """Reject wire."""
    global WS_WIRE_STATUS, REJECT_WIRE_REF, WS_WIRE_REJECT, REJECT_REASON, WS_PROCESS_DATE, REJECT_DATE, WS_NOTIF_TYPE
    WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'REJECTED'
    # initialize WS_WIRE_REJECT_REC
    REJECT_WIRE_REF  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_REF
    REJECT_REASON  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_REJECT
    REJECT_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    # write WIRE_REJECT_RECORD from WS_WIRE_REJECT_REC
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'wire_rejected'
    send_notification_C11()

def ach_processing_C11() -> None:
    """ACH processing."""
    receive_ach_file_C11()
    validate_ach_entries_C11()
    process_ach_credits_C11()
    process_ach_debits_C11()
    generate_ach_return_C11()

def receive_ach_file_C11() -> None:
    """Receive ACH file."""
    global ACH_INPUT_FILE, WS_ACH_FILE_HEADER, ACH_FILE_ID, ACH_CREATION_DATE, ACH_ENTRY_COUNT, WS_CURRENT_ACH_FILE, WS_ACH_FILE_DATE, WS_EXPECTED_ENTRIES
    # open input ACH_INPUT_FILE
    # read ACH_INPUT_FILE into WS_ACH_FILE_HEADER
    WS_CURRENT_ACH_FILE  = None  # TODO: was ACH_FILE_ID
    WS_ACH_FILE_DATE  = None  # TODO: was ACH_CREATION_DATE
    WS_EXPECTED_ENTRIES  = None  # TODO: was ACH_ENTRY_COUNT

def validate_ach_entries_C11() -> None:
    """Validate ACH entries."""
    global WS_VALID_ENTRIES, WS_INVALID_ENTRIES, WS_EOF_FLAG
    WS_VALID_ENTRIES = 0
    WS_INVALID_ENTRIES = 0
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        try:
            # read ACH_INPUT_FILE into WS_ACH_ENTRY
            pass
        except StopIteration:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            validate_single_entry_C11()
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def validate_single_entry_C11() -> None:
    """Validate single entry."""
    global WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID, ACH_ROUTING, WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE, ACH_ACCOUNT, ACH_AMOUNT, WS_VALID_ENTRIES, WS_INVALID_ENTRIES
    WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'Y'
    if not ACH_ROUTING.isnumeric():
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R03'
    if ACH_ACCOUNT == " ":
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'
    if ACH_AMOUNT <= 0:
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R06'
    if WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID == 'Y':
        WS_VALID_ENTRIES += 1
    else:
        WS_INVALID_ENTRIES += 1

def process_ach_credits_C11() -> None:
    """Process ACH credits."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, ACH_TRANS_CODE
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        try:
            # read ACH_INPUT_FILE into WS_ACH_ENTRY
            pass
        except StopIteration:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            if ACH_TRANS_CODE in ['22', '23', '32', '33']:
                apply_credit_C11()
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def apply_credit_C11() -> None:
    """Apply credit."""
    global ACH_ACCOUNT, WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_FOUND_FLAG, ACH_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_CREDITS_POSTED, WS_TOTAL_CREDITS, WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE
    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was ACH_ACCOUNT
    search_account_C11()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'Y':
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
        update_account_C11()
        WS_CREDITS_POSTED += 1
        WS_TOTAL_CREDITS += None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
    else:
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'
        create_return_entry_C11()

def process_ach_debits_C11() -> None:
    """Process ACH debits."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, ACH_TRANS_CODE
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        try:
            # read ACH_INPUT_FILE into WS_ACH_ENTRY
            pass
        except StopIteration:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            if ACH_TRANS_CODE in ['27', '28', '37', '38']:
                apply_debit_C11()
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def apply_debit_C11() -> None:
    """Apply debit."""
    global ACH_ACCOUNT, WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_FOUND_FLAG, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, ACH_AMOUNT, WS_DEBITS_POSTED, WS_TOTAL_DEBITS, WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE
    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was ACH_ACCOUNT
    search_account_C11()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'Y':
        if WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE >= ACH_AMOUNT:
            WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
            update_account_C11()
            WS_DEBITS_POSTED += 1
            WS_TOTAL_DEBITS += None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
        else:
            WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R01'
            create_return_entry_C11()
    else:
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'
        create_return_entry_C11()

def generate_ach_return_C11() -> None:
    """Generate ACH return."""
    global WS_RETURN_COUNT
    if WS_RETURN_COUNT > 0:
        create_return_file_C11()

def create_return_entry_C11() -> None:
    """Create return entry."""
    global WS_ACH_RETURN_ENTRY, ACH_TRACE_NUMBER, RETURN_ORIG_TRACE, WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE, RETURN_CODE, ACH_AMOUNT, RETURN_AMOUNT, ACH_ACCOUNT, RETURN_ACCOUNT, WS_RETURN_COUNT
    # initialize WS_ACH_RETURN_ENTRY
    RETURN_ORIG_TRACE  = None  # TODO: was ACH_TRACE_NUMBER
    RETURN_CODE  = None  # TODO: was WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE
    RETURN_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
    RETURN_ACCOUNT  = None  # TODO: was ACH_ACCOUNT
    WS_RETURN_COUNT += 1
    # write ACH_RETURN_RECORD from WS_ACH_RETURN_ENTRY

def create_return_file_C11() -> None:
    """Create return file."""
    global ACH_RETURN_FILE
    # open output ACH_RETURN_FILE
    write_return_header_C11()
    write_return_entries_C11()
    write_return_trailer_C11()
    # close ACH_RETURN_FILE

def write_return_header_C11() -> None:
    """Write return header."""
    global WS_RETURN_HEADER, RETURN_RECORD_TYPE, WS_OUR_ROUTING, RETURN_IMMEDIATE_DEST, WS_OUR_COMPANY_ID, RETURN_IMMEDIATE_ORIGIN, RETURN_FILE_DATE
    # initialize WS_RETURN_HEADER
    RETURN_RECORD_TYPE = '1'
    RETURN_IMMEDIATE_DEST  = None  # TODO: was WS_OUR_ROUTING
    RETURN_IMMEDIATE_ORIGIN  = None  # TODO: was WS_OUR_COMPANY_ID
    # RETURN_FILE_DATE = FUNCTION current_date
    # write ACH_RETURN_RECORD from WS_RETURN_HEADER

def write_return_entries_C11() -> None:
    """Write return entries."""
    global WS_RETURN_IDX, WS_RETURN_COUNT
    WS_RETURN_IDX = 0
    while WS_RETURN_IDX > WS_RETURN_COUNT:
        # write ACH_RETURN_RECORD from WS_RETURN_ENTRY(WS_RETURN_IDX)
        WS_RETURN_IDX += 1

def write_return_trailer_C11() -> None:
    """Write return trailer."""
    global WS_RETURN_TRAILER, RETURN_RECORD_TYPE, WS_RETURN_COUNT, RETURN_ENTRY_COUNT, WS_RETURN_TOTAL, RETURN_TOTAL_AMOUNT
    # initialize WS_RETURN_TRAILER
    RETURN_RECORD_TYPE = '9'
    RETURN_ENTRY_COUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_RETURN_COUNT
    RETURN_TOTAL_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_RETURN_TOTAL
    # write ACH_RETURN_RECORD from WS_RETURN_TRAILER

def statement_generation_C11() -> None:
    """Statement generation."""
    prepare_statement_data_C11()
    generate_account_summary_C11()
    generate_transaction_detail_C11()
    calculate_statement_totals_C11()
    format_statement_C11()
    deliver_statement_C11()

    # WS_STMT_DATE = FUNCTION current_date
    # WS_STMT_START_DATE = FUNCTION integer_of_date(WS_STMT_DATE) - 30
    WS_STMT_END_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_STMT_DATE
    WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT = 0
    WS_STMT_CREDIT_TOTAL = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_STMT_DEBIT_TOTAL = Decimal("0.00")

def generate_account_summary_C11() -> None:
    """Generate account summary."""
    global WS_STMT_SUMMARY, ACCT_ID, STMT_ACCOUNT_NUMBER, ACCT_TYPE, STMT_ACCOUNT_TYPE, ACCT_OWNER_NAME, STMT_CUSTOMER_NAME, ACCT_OWNER_ADDRESS, STMT_CUSTOMER_ADDR, WS_OPENING_BALANCE, STMT_OPENING_BAL, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, STMT_CLOSING_BAL
    # initialize WS_STMT_SUMMARY
    STMT_ACCOUNT_NUMBER  = None  # TODO: was ACCT_ID
    STMT_ACCOUNT_TYPE  = None  # TODO: was ACCT_TYPE
    STMT_CUSTOMER_NAME  = None  # TODO: was ACCT_OWNER_NAME
    STMT_CUSTOMER_ADDR  = None  # TODO: was ACCT_OWNER_ADDRESS
    STMT_OPENING_BAL  = None  # TODO: was WS_OPENING_BALANCE
    STMT_CLOSING_BAL  = None  # TODO: was WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE

def generate_transaction_detail_C11() -> None:
    """Generate transaction detail."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, ACCT_ID, HIST_ACCOUNT, HIST_DATE, WS_STMT_START_DATE
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        try:
            # read TRANSACTION_HISTORY into WS_TRANS_HIST_REC
            pass
        except StopIteration:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            if HIST_ACCOUNT == ACCT_ID:
                if HIST_DATE >= WS_STMT_START_DATE:
                    add_transaction_line_C11()
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def add_transaction_line_C11() -> None:
    """Add transaction line."""
    global WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT, HIST_DATE, STMT_TRANS_DATE, HIST_DESC, STMT_TRANS_DESC, HIST_AMOUNT, STMT_TRANS_AMT, HIST_BALANCE, STMT_TRANS_BAL, HIST_TYPE, WS_STMT_CREDIT_TOTAL, WS_STMT_DEBIT_TOTAL
    WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT += 1
    STMT_TRANS_DATE.append(str(HIST_DATE))
    STMT_TRANS_DESC.append(HIST_DESC)
    STMT_TRANS_AMT.append(HIST_AMOUNT)
    STMT_TRANS_BAL.append(HIST_BALANCE)
    if HIST_TYPE == 'C':
        WS_STMT_CREDIT_TOTAL += None  # TODO: was HIST_AMOUNT
    else:
        WS_STMT_DEBIT_TOTAL += None  # TODO: was HIST_AMOUNT

def calculate_statement_totals_C11() -> None:
    """Calculate statement totals."""
    global WS_STMT_CREDIT_TOTAL, STMT_TOTAL_CREDITS, WS_STMT_DEBIT_TOTAL, STMT_TOTAL_DEBITS, STMT_NET_CHANGE, WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT, STMT_TRANS_COUNT, WS_TOTAL_DAILY_BALANCES, STMT_AVG_DAILY_BAL
    STMT_TOTAL_CREDITS = WS_STMT_CREDIT_TOTAL
    STMT_TOTAL_DEBITS  = None  # TODO: was WS_STMT_DEBIT_TOTAL
    STMT_NET_CHANGE = WS_STMT_CREDIT_TOTAL - WS_STMT_DEBIT_TOTAL
    STMT_TRANS_COUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT
    if WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT > 0:
        STMT_AVG_DAILY_BAL = WS_TOTAL_DAILY_BALANCES / 30

def format_statement_C11() -> None:
    """Format statement."""
    create_header_C11()
    create_summary_section_C11()
    create_transaction_list_C11()
    create_footer_C11()

def create_header_C11() -> None:
    """Create header."""
    global WS_STMT_LINE, WS_STMT_DATE, STATEMENT_RECORD
    WS_STMT_LINE = " "
    WS_STMT_LINE = f'ACCOUNT STATEMENT - {WS_STMT_DATE}'
    # write STATEMENT_RECORD from WS_STMT_LINE
    WS_STMT_LINE = '-' * len(WS_STMT_LINE)
    # write STATEMENT_RECORD from WS_STMT_LINE

def create_summary_section_C11() -> None:
    """Create summary section."""
    global STMT_ACCOUNT_NUMBER, WS_STMT_LINE, STATEMENT_RECORD, STMT_CUSTOMER_NAME, STMT_OPENING_BAL, STMT_CLOSING_BAL
    WS_STMT_LINE = f'Account: {STMT_ACCOUNT_NUMBER}'
    # write STATEMENT_RECORD from WS_STMT_LINE
    WS_STMT_LINE =None  # TODO: Add value

def validate_stop_request_C12() -> None:
    """Validates a stop request."""
    global WS_STOP_VALID_C12, WS_CHECK_NUMBER_C12, WS_STOP_REJECT_C12, WS_CHECK_ALREADY_CLEARED_C12
    WS_STOP_VALID_C12 = 'Y'
    if WS_CHECK_NUMBER_C12 == 0:
        WS_STOP_VALID_C12 = 'N'
        WS_STOP_REJECT_C12 = 'CHECK NUMBER REQUIRED'
    if WS_CHECK_ALREADY_CLEARED_C12 == 'Y':
        WS_STOP_VALID_C12 = 'N'
        WS_STOP_REJECT_C12 = 'CHECK ALREADY CLEARED'

def create_stop_order_C12() -> None:
    """Creates a stop order."""
    global WS_STOP_RECORD_C12, ACCT_ID_C12, WS_CHECK_NUMBER_C12, WS_CHECK_AMOUNT_C12, WS_PAYEE_NAME_C12, WS_PROCESS_DATE_C12, STOP_ACCOUNT_C12, STOP_CHECK_NUMBER_C12, STOP_AMOUNT_C12, STOP_PAYEE_C12, STOP_EFFECTIVE_DATE_C12, STOP_EXPIRY_DATE_C12, STOP_STATUS_C12, STOP_RECORD_C12
    WS_STOP_RECORD_C12 = WsStopRecordC12()
    STOP_ACCOUNT_C12  = None  # TODO: was ACCT_ID_C12
    STOP_CHECK_NUMBER_C12  = None  # TODO: was WS_CHECK_NUMBER_C12
    STOP_AMOUNT_C12  = None  # TODO: was WS_CHECK_AMOUNT_C12
    STOP_PAYEE_C12  = None  # TODO: was WS_PAYEE_NAME_C12
    STOP_EFFECTIVE_DATE_C12  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE_C12
    STOP_EXPIRY_DATE_C12 = int(0 + 180)
    STOP_STATUS_C12 = 'A'
    STOP_RECORD_C12 = str(WS_STOP_RECORD_C12)

def apply_stop_fee_C12() -> None:
    """Applies a stop fee."""
    global WS_STOP_PAYMENT_FEE_C12, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C12, WS_NOTIF_TYPE_C12, WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL_C12, WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT_C12, WS_CHECK_NUMBER_C12
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C12 -= WS_STOP_PAYMENT_FEE_C12
    update_account_C12()
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE_C12 = 'stop_payment'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL_C12 = 'EMAIL'
    WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT_C12 = f'Stop payment placed on check # {WS_CHECK_NUMBER_C12}'
    send_notification_C12()

def safe_deposit_box_C12() -> None:
    """Handles safe deposit box procedures."""
    box_rental_C12()
    box_access_C12()
    box_drilling_C12()
    box_billing_C12()

def box_rental_C12() -> None:
    """Handles box rental."""
    global WS_RENTAL_REQUEST_C12, WS_BOX_AVAILABLE_C12
    if WS_RENTAL_REQUEST_C12 == 'Y':
        check_availability_C12()
        if WS_BOX_AVAILABLE_C12 == 'Y':
            assign_box_C12()
            create_rental_agreement_C12()

def check_availability_C12() -> None:
    """Checks box availability."""
    global WS_BOX_AVAILABLE_C12, WS_BOX_IDX_C12, WS_TOTAL_BOXES_C12, WS_REQUESTED_SIZE_C12, WS_ASSIGNED_BOX_C12
    WS_BOX_AVAILABLE_C12 = 'N'
    WS_BOX_IDX_C12 = 1
    while WS_BOX_IDX_C12 <= WS_TOTAL_BOXES_C12:
        box_status = "" #box_status(ws_box_idx)
        box_size = "" #box_size(ws_box_idx)
        if box_status == 'A':
            if box_size == WS_REQUESTED_SIZE_C12:
                WS_BOX_AVAILABLE_C12 = 'Y'
                WS_ASSIGNED_BOX_C12  = None  # TODO: was WS_BOX_IDX_C12
                break
        WS_BOX_IDX_C12 += 1

def assign_box_C12() -> None:
    """Assigns a box."""
    global WS_ASSIGNED_BOX_C12, WS_CUSTOMER_ID_C12, WS_PROCESS_DATE_C12
    box_status = "" #box_status(ws_assigned_box)
    box_renter = "" #box_renter(ws_assigned_box)
    box_rental_date = "" #box_rental_date(ws_assigned_box)

    box_status = 'R'
    box_renter  = None  # TODO: was WS_CUSTOMER_ID_C12
    box_rental_date  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE_C12

def create_rental_agreement_C12() -> None:
    """Creates a rental agreement."""
    global WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT_C12, WS_ASSIGNED_BOX_C12, WS_CUSTOMER_ID_C12, WS_PROCESS_DATE_C12, RENTAL_BOX_NUMBER_C12, RENTAL_CUSTOMER_C12, RENTAL_START_DATE_C12, RENTAL_ANNUAL_FEE_C12, RENTAL_RECORD_C12, WS_BOX_SIZE_FEE_C12, WS_REQUESTED_SIZE_C12
    WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT_C12 = WsRentalAgreementC12()
    RENTAL_BOX_NUMBER_C12  = None  # TODO: was WS_ASSIGNED_BOX_C12
    RENTAL_CUSTOMER_C12  = None  # TODO: was WS_CUSTOMER_ID_C12
    RENTAL_START_DATE_C12  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE_C12
    RENTAL_ANNUAL_FEE_C12 = WS_BOX_SIZE_FEE_C12[0] #WS_BOX_SIZE_FEE(ws_requested_size)
    RENTAL_RECORD_C12 = str(WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT_C12)

def box_access_C12() -> None:
    """Handles box access."""
    global WS_ACCESS_REQUEST_C12, WS_RENTER_VERIFIED_C12
    if WS_ACCESS_REQUEST_C12 == 'Y':
        verify_renter_C12()
        if WS_RENTER_VERIFIED_C12 == 'Y':
            log_access_C12()
            escort_to_vault_C12()

def verify_renter_C12() -> None:
    """Verifies the renter."""
    global WS_RENTER_VERIFIED_C12, WS_BOX_NUMBER_C12, WS_CUSTOMER_ID_C12, WS_ID_VERIFIED_C12, WS_KEY_VERIFIED_C12
    WS_RENTER_VERIFIED_C12 = 'N'
    box_renter = "" #box_renter(ws_box_number)

    if box_renter == WS_CUSTOMER_ID_C12:
        if WS_ID_VERIFIED_C12 == 'Y':
            if WS_KEY_VERIFIED_C12 == 'Y':
                WS_RENTER_VERIFIED_C12 = 'Y'

def log_access_C12() -> None:
    """Logs box access."""
    global WS_ACCESS_LOG_C12, WS_BOX_NUMBER_C12, WS_CUSTOMER_ID_C12, WS_PROCESS_DATE_C12, ACCESS_BOX_NUMBER_C12, ACCESS_CUSTOMER_C12, ACCESS_DATE_C12, ACCESS_TIME_C12, ACCESS_TYPE_C12, ACCESS_LOG_RECORD_C12
    WS_ACCESS_LOG_C12 = WsAccessLogC12()
    ACCESS_BOX_NUMBER_C12  = None  # TODO: was WS_BOX_NUMBER_C12
    ACCESS_CUSTOMER_C12  = None  # TODO: was WS_CUSTOMER_ID_C12
    ACCESS_DATE_C12  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE_C12
    ACCESS_TIME_C12 = "0000" #FUNCTION current_time
    ACCESS_TYPE_C12 = 'ENTRY'
    ACCESS_LOG_RECORD_C12 = str(WS_ACCESS_LOG_C12)

def escort_to_vault_C12() -> None:
    """Escorts to vault."""
    global WS_DISPLAY_MSG_C12
    WS_DISPLAY_MSG_C12 = 'VAULT ACCESS GRANTED'
    print(WS_DISPLAY_MSG_C12)

def box_drilling_C12() -> None:
    """Handles box drilling."""
    global WS_DRILLING_REQUEST_C12, WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED_C12
    if WS_DRILLING_REQUEST_C12 == 'Y':
        validate_drilling_auth_C12()
        if WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED_C12 == 'Y':
            schedule_drilling_C12()
            notify_renter_C12()

def validate_drilling_auth_C12() -> None:
    """Validates drilling authorization."""
    global WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED_C12, WS_RENT_DELINQUENT_MONTHS_C12, WS_COURT_ORDER_C12, WS_DECEASED_RENTER_C12, WS_EXECUTOR_VERIFIED_C12
    WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED_C12 = 'N'
    if WS_RENT_DELINQUENT_MONTHS_C12 >= 12:
        WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED_C12 = 'Y'
    if WS_COURT_ORDER_C12 == 'Y':
        WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED_C12 = 'Y'
    if WS_DECEASED_RENTER_C12 == 'Y':
        if WS_EXECUTOR_VERIFIED_C12 == 'Y':
            WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED_C12 = 'Y'

def schedule_drilling_C12() -> None:
    """Schedules drilling."""
    global WS_DRILLING_RECORD_C12, WS_BOX_NUMBER_C12, WS_DRILLING_REASON_C12, DRILL_BOX_NUMBER_C12, DRILL_REASON_C12, DRILL_SCHEDULED_DATE_C12, DRILLING_RECORD_C12, WS_PROCESS_DATE_C12
    WS_DRILLING_RECORD_C12 = WsDrillingRecordC12()
    DRILL_BOX_NUMBER_C12  = None  # TODO: was WS_BOX_NUMBER_C12
    DRILL_REASON_C12 = WS_DRILLING_REASON_C12
    DRILL_SCHEDULED_DATE_C12 = int(0 + 30)
    DRILLING_RECORD_C12 = str(WS_DRILLING_RECORD_C12)

def notify_renter_C12() -> None:
    """Notifies the renter."""
    global WS_NOTIF_TYPE_C12, WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL_C12, WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT_C12
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE_C12 = 'box_drilling'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL_C12 = 'MAIL'
    WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT_C12 = 'Important notice regarding your safe deposit box'
    send_notification_C12()

def box_billing_C12() -> None:
    """Handles box billing."""
    global WS_BOX_IDX_C12, WS_TOTAL_BOXES_C12, WS_CUSTOMER_ID_C12, WS_FEE_AMOUNT_C12, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C12
    WS_BOX_IDX_C12 = 1
    while WS_BOX_IDX_C12 <= WS_TOTAL_BOXES_C12:
        box_status = "" #box_status(ws_box_idx)
        box_renewal_due = "" #box_renewal_due(ws_box_idx)
        box_renter = "" #box_renter(ws_box_idx)
        box_annual_fee = Decimal("0.00") #box_annual_fee(ws_box_idx)
        if box_status == 'R':
            if box_renewal_due == 'Y':
                WS_CUSTOMER_ID_C12 = box_renter
                WS_FEE_AMOUNT_C12 = box_annual_fee
                WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C12 -= None  # TODO: was WS_FEE_AMOUNT_C12
                update_account_C12()
                box_next_renewal = 0 #box_next_renewal(ws_box_idx)
                box_next_renewal = box_next_renewal + 10000
        WS_BOX_IDX_C12 += 1

def charge_annual_fee_C12() -> None:
    """Charges the annual fee."""
    pass

def merchant_services_C12() -> None:
    """Handles merchant services."""
    process_authorization_C12()
    capture_transaction_C12()
    process_settlement_C12()
    handle_chargeback_C12()

def process_authorization_C12() -> None:
    """Processes authorization."""
    global WS_CARD_VALID_C12, WS_FRAUD_APPROVED_C12, WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE_C12
    validate_card_C12()
    if WS_CARD_VALID_C12 == 'Y':
        check_fraud_score_C12()
        if WS_FRAUD_APPROVED_C12 == 'Y':
            check_available_credit_C12()
            if WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE_C12 == 'Y':
                approve_auth_C12()
            else:
                decline_auth_C12()
        else:
            decline_auth_C12()
    else:
        decline_auth_C12()

def validate_card_C12() -> None:
    """Validates a card."""
    global WS_CARD_VALID_C12, WS_LUHN_VALID_C12, WS_NOT_EXPIRED_C12, WS_CVV_VALID_C12
    WS_CARD_VALID_C12 = 'N'
    check_luhn_C12()
    if WS_LUHN_VALID_C12 == 'Y':
        check_expiry_C12()
        if WS_NOT_EXPIRED_C12 == 'Y':
            check_cvv_C12()
            if WS_CVV_VALID_C12 == 'Y':
                WS_CARD_VALID_C12 = 'Y'

def check_luhn_C12() -> None:
    """Checks the Luhn algorithm."""
    global WS_LUHN_SUM_C12, WS_LUHN_IDX_C12, WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER_C12, WS_LUHN_DIGIT_C12, WS_LUHN_VALID_C12
    WS_LUHN_SUM_C12 = 0
    WS_LUHN_IDX_C12 = 16
    while WS_LUHN_IDX_C12 >= 1:
        WS_LUHN_DIGIT_C12 = int(WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER_C12[0]) #ws_auth_card_number(ws_luhn_idx:1)
        if (17 - WS_LUHN_IDX_C12) % 2 == 0:
            WS_LUHN_DIGIT_C12 *= 2
            if WS_LUHN_DIGIT_C12 > 9:
                WS_LUHN_DIGIT_C12 -= 9
        WS_LUHN_SUM_C12 += None  # TODO: was WS_LUHN_DIGIT_C12
        WS_LUHN_IDX_C12 -= 1
    if WS_LUHN_SUM_C12 % 10 == 0:
        WS_LUHN_VALID_C12 = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_LUHN_VALID_C12 = 'N'

def check_expiry_C12() -> None:
    """Checks the expiry date."""
    global WS_AUTH_EXPIRY_DATE_C12, WS_PROCESS_DATE_C12, WS_NOT_EXPIRED_C12
    if WS_AUTH_EXPIRY_DATE_C12 >= WS_PROCESS_DATE_C12:
        WS_NOT_EXPIRED_C12 = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_NOT_EXPIRED_C12 = 'N'

def error_message_C13(ws_file_result: str, file_err_msg: str, file_err_timestamp: str) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    file_err_msg = ws_file_result
    file_err_timestamp = current_date_C13()
    write_file_error_C13(ws_file_error_log: str)

def logging_utilities_C13() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    log_info_C13()
    log_warning_C13()
    log_error_C13()

def log_info_C13() -> None:
    """Log info message."""
    log_level: str = 'INFO'
    log_message: str = ws_log_message
    log_timestamp: str = current_date_C13()
    write_log_record_C13(ws_log_entry: str)

    """Log warning message."""
    log_level: str = 'WARN'
    log_message: str = ws_log_message
    log_timestamp: str = current_date_C13()
    write_log_record_C13(ws_log_entry: str)

def log_error_C13() -> None:
    """Log error message."""
    log_level: str = 'ERROR'
    log_message: str = ws_log_message
    log_timestamp: str = current_date_C13()
    write_log_record_C13(ws_log_entry: str)

def error_handling_C13() -> None:
    """Handle errors."""
    format_error_C13()
    display_error_C13()
    write_error_log_C13()

def format_error_C13() -> None:
    """Format error message."""
    ws_formatted_error: str = f"ERROR: {ws_error_code} - {ws_error_msg}"

def display_error_C13() -> None:
    """Display error message."""
    print(ws_formatted_error)

def write_error_log_C13() -> None:
    """Write error log."""
    initialize_error_log_C13()
    err_log_code: str = ws_error_code
    err_log_msg: str = ws_error_msg
    err_log_timestamp: str = current_date_C13()
    err_log_program: str = ws_program_name
    err_log_paragraph: str = ws_paragraph_name
    write_error_log_record_C13(ws_error_log_rec: str)

def treasury_management_C13() -> None:
    """Manage treasury functions."""
    calculate_cash_position_C13()
    project_cash_flows_C13()
    manage_reserves_C13()
    manage_investments_C13()
    manage_borrowings_C13()

def calculate_cash_position_C13() -> None:
    """Calculate cash position."""
    ws_cash_position: Decimal = Decimal('0')
    sum_vault_cash_C13()
    sum_fed_account_C13()
    sum_correspondent_balances_C13()

def sum_vault_cash_C13() -> None:
    """Sum vault cash."""
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_vault_rec: str = read_vault_cash_file_C13()
            vault_balance: Decimal = get_vault_balance_C13(ws_vault_rec)
            ws_cash_position += vault_balance
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def sum_fed_account_C13() -> None:
    """Sum fed account balance."""
    ws_fed_balance: Decimal = read_fed_account_file_C13()
    ws_cash_position += ws_fed_balance

def sum_correspondent_balances_C13() -> None:
    """Sum correspondent balances."""
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_corr_rec: str = read_correspondent_file_C13()
            corr_balance: Decimal = get_corr_balance_C13(ws_corr_rec)
            ws_cash_position += corr_balance
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def project_cash_flows_C13() -> None:
    """Project cash flows."""
    ws_projected_inflows: Decimal = Decimal('0')
    ws_projected_outflows: Decimal = Decimal('0')
    project_loan_payments_C13()
    project_deposit_flows_C13()
    project_investment_maturities_C13()
    ws_net_position: Decimal = ws_cash_position + ws_projected_inflows - ws_projected_outflows

def project_loan_payments_C13() -> None:
    """Project loan payments."""
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_loan_pmt_rec: str = read_loan_schedule_file_C13()
            loan_pmt_date: int = get_loan_pmt_date_C13(ws_loan_pmt_rec)
            loan_pmt_amount: Decimal = get_loan_pmt_amount_C13(ws_loan_pmt_rec)
            if loan_pmt_date <= ws_projection_date:
                ws_projected_inflows += loan_pmt_amount
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def project_deposit_flows_C13() -> None:
    """Project deposit flows."""
    ws_expected_deposits: Decimal = ws_avg_daily_deposits * ws_projection_days
    ws_expected_withdrawals: Decimal = ws_avg_daily_withdrawals * ws_projection_days
    ws_projected_inflows += ws_expected_deposits
    ws_projected_outflows += ws_expected_withdrawals

def project_investment_maturities_C13() -> None:
    """Project investment maturities."""
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_inv_rec: str = read_investment_file_C13()
            inv_maturity_date: int = get_inv_maturity_date_C13(ws_inv_rec)
            inv_par_value: Decimal = get_inv_par_value_C13(ws_inv_rec)
            if inv_maturity_date <= ws_projection_date:
                ws_projected_inflows += inv_par_value
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def manage_reserves_C13() -> None:
    """Manage reserves."""
    calculate_reserve_requirement_C13()
    check_reserve_position_C13()
    if ws_reserve_deficiency == 'Y':
        cover_reserve_shortfall_C13()
    else:
        invest_excess_reserves_C13()

def calculate_reserve_requirement_C13() -> None:
    """Calculate reserve requirement."""
    ws_reserve_requirement: Decimal = ws_total_deposits * ws_reserve_ratio

def check_reserve_position_C13() -> None:
    """Check reserve position."""
    ws_excess_reserves: Decimal = ws_fed_balance - ws_reserve_requirement
    if ws_excess_reserves < Decimal('0'):
        ws_reserve_deficiency: str = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_reserve_deficiency: str = 'N'

def cover_reserve_shortfall_C13() -> None:
    """Cover reserve shortfall."""
    ws_shortfall_amount: Decimal = Decimal('0') - ws_excess_reserves
    borrow_fed_funds_C13()

def borrow_fed_funds_C13() -> None:
    """Borrow fed funds."""
    ws_fed_funds_transaction: str = initialize_fed_funds_transaction_C13()
    ff_trans_type: str = 'BORROW'
    ff_amount: Decimal = ws_shortfall_amount
    ff_rate: Decimal = ws_fed_funds_rate
    ff_settle_date: int = ws_process_date
    ff_maturity_date: int = integer_of_date_C13(ws_process_date) + 1
    write_fed_funds_record_C13(ws_fed_funds_transaction: str)

def invest_excess_reserves_C13() -> None:
    """Invest excess reserves."""
    if ws_excess_reserves > ws_min_invest_amount:
        sell_fed_funds_C13()

def sell_fed_funds_C13() -> None:
    """Sell fed funds."""
    ws_fed_funds_transaction: str = initialize_fed_funds_transaction_C13()
    ff_trans_type: str = 'SELL'
    ff_amount: Decimal = ws_excess_reserves
    ff_rate: Decimal = ws_fed_funds_rate
    ff_settle_date: int = ws_process_date
    ff_maturity_date: int = integer_of_date_C13(ws_process_date) + 1
    write_fed_funds_record_C13(ws_fed_funds_transaction: str)

def manage_investments_C13() -> None:
    """Manage investments."""
    review_investment_portfolio_C13()
    execute_investment_strategy_C13()
    mark_to_market_C13()

def review_investment_portfolio_C13() -> None:
    """Review investment portfolio."""
    ws_investment_pool: Decimal = Decimal('0')
    ws_avg_yield: Decimal = Decimal('0')
    ws_avg_duration: Decimal = Decimal('0')
    ws_total_yield: Decimal = Decimal('0')
    ws_total_duration: Decimal = Decimal('0')
    ws_inv_count: int = 0
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_inv_rec: str = read_investment_file_C13()
            inv_market_value: Decimal = get_inv_market_value_C13(ws_inv_rec)
            inv_yield: Decimal = get_inv_yield_C13(ws_inv_rec)
            inv_duration: Decimal = get_inv_duration_C13(ws_inv_rec)
            ws_investment_pool += inv_market_value
            ws_total_yield += inv_yield
            ws_total_duration += inv_duration
            ws_inv_count += 1
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    if ws_inv_count > 0:
        ws_avg_yield = ws_total_yield / ws_inv_count
        ws_avg_duration = ws_total_duration / ws_inv_count
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def execute_investment_strategy_C13() -> None:
    """Execute investment strategy."""
    if ws_rate_outlook == 'RISING':
        shorten_duration_C13()
    elif ws_rate_outlook == 'FALLING':
        extend_duration_C13()
    elif ws_rate_outlook == 'STABLE':
        maintain_position_C13()

def shorten_duration_C13() -> None:
    """Shorten portfolio duration."""
    print('STRATEGY: SHORTENING PORTFOLIO DURATION')

def extend_duration_C13() -> None:
    """Extend portfolio duration."""
    print('STRATEGY: EXTENDING PORTFOLIO DURATION')

def maintain_position_C13() -> None:
    """Maintain current position."""
    print('STRATEGY: MAINTAINING CURRENT POSITION')

def mark_to_market_C13() -> None:
    """Mark to market investments."""
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_inv_rec: str = read_investment_file_C13()
            inv_cusip: str = get_inv_cusip_C13(ws_inv_rec)
            get_market_price_C13(inv_cusip: str)
            inv_par_value: Decimal = get_inv_par_value_C13(ws_inv_rec)
            inv_market_value: Decimal = inv_par_value * ws_market_price / Decimal('100')
            inv_book_value: Decimal = get_inv_book_value_C13(ws_inv_rec)
            inv_unrealized_gl: Decimal = inv_market_value - inv_book_value
            rewrite_investment_record_C13(ws_inv_rec: str)
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def get_market_price_C13(inv_cusip: str) -> None:
    """Get market price for a bond."""
    ws_cusip_lookup: str = inv_cusip
    ws_market_price: Decimal = bondprice_C13(ws_cusip_lookup: str)

def manage_borrowings_C13() -> None:
    """Manage borrowings."""
    review_borrowing_capacity_C13()
    optimize_funding_mix_C13()
    manage_maturities_C13()

def review_borrowing_capacity_C13() -> None:
    """Review borrowing capacity."""
    ws_borrowing_capacity: Decimal = Decimal('0')
    ws_borrowing_capacity += ws_fhlb_capacity
    ws_borrowing_capacity += ws_repo_capacity
    ws_borrowing_capacity += ws_credit_line_avail

def optimize_funding_mix_C13() -> None:
    """Optimize funding mix."""
    ws_deposit_cost: Decimal = ws_total_int_expense / ws_total_deposits * Decimal('100')
    if ws_deposit_cost > ws_wholesale_rate:
        print('CONSIDER WHOLESALE FUNDING')

def manage_maturities_C13() -> None:
    """Manage borrowing maturities."""
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_borrow_rec: str = read_borrowing_file_C13()
            borrow_maturity: int = get_borrow_maturity_C13(ws_borrow_rec)
            if borrow_maturity <= ws_process_date + 7:
                rollover_decision_C13(borrow_rec: str = ws_borrow_rec)
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def rollover_decision_C13(borrow_rec: str) -> None:
    """Decide whether to repay or rollover borrowing."""
    borrow_amount: Decimal = get_borrow_amount_C13(borrow_rec)
    if ws_cash_position >= borrow_amount:
        repay_borrowing_C13(borrow_rec: str)
    else:
        rollover_borrowing_C13(borrow_rec: str)

def repay_borrowing_C13(borrow_rec: str) -> None:
    """Repay borrowing."""
    borrow_amount: Decimal = get_borrow_amount_C13(borrow_rec)
    ws_cash_position -= borrow_amount
    borrow_status: str = 'REPAID'
    rewrite_borrowing_record_C13(ws_borrow_rec: str)

def rollover_borrowing_C13(borrow_rec: str) -> None:
    """Rollover borrowing."""
    borrow_rollover_date: int = ws_process_date
    borrow_maturity: int = integer_of_date_C13(ws_process_date) + 30
    borrow_rate: Decimal = ws_current_rate
    rewrite_borrowing_record_C13(ws_borrow_rec: str)

def liquidity_management_C13() -> None:
    """Manage liquidity."""
    calculate_liquidity_ratios_C13()
    monitor_liquidity_limits_C13()
    contingency_funding_plan_C13()

def calculate_liquidity_ratios_C13() -> None:
    """Calculate liquidity ratios."""
    calculate_lcr_C13()
    calculate_nsfr_C13()
    calculate_basic_ratio_C13()

def calculate_lcr_C13() -> None:
    """Calculate LCR."""
    sum_hqla_C13()
    calculate_net_outflows_C13()
    if ws_lcr_denominator > Decimal('0'):
        ws_lcr_ratio: Decimal = (ws_lcr_numerator / ws_lcr_denominator) * Decimal('100')

def sum_hqla_C13() -> None:
    """Sum High Quality Liquid Assets."""
    ws_lcr_numerator: Decimal = Decimal('0')
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_inv_rec: str = read_investment_file_C13()
            inv_hqla_level: str = get_inv_hqla_level_C13(ws_inv_rec)
            inv_market_value: Decimal = get_inv_market_value_C13(ws_inv_rec)
            if inv_hqla_level == '1':
                ws_lcr_numerator += inv_market_value
            elif inv_hqla_level == '2A':
                ws_adjusted_value: Decimal = inv_market_value * Decimal('0.85')
                ws_lcr_numerator += ws_adjusted_value
            elif inv_hqla_level == '2B':
                ws_adjusted_value: Decimal = inv_market_value * Decimal('0.50')
                ws_lcr_numerator += ws_adjusted_value
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def calculate_net_outflows_C13() -> None:
    """Calculate net outflows for LCR."""
    ws_total_outflows: Decimal = Decimal('0')
    ws_total_inflows: Decimal = Decimal('0')
    ws_retail_outflow: Decimal = ws_stable_deposits * Decimal('0.03') + ws_less_stable_deposits * Decimal('0.10')
    ws_wholesale_outflow: Decimal = ws_operational_deposits * Decimal('0.25') + ws_non_operational * Decimal('0.40')
    ws_total_outflows += ws_retail_outflow
    ws_total_outflows += ws_wholesale_outflow
    ws_lcr_denominator: Decimal = ws_total_outflows - min(ws_total_inflows, ws_total_outflows * Decimal('0.75'))

def calculate_nsfr_C13() -> None:
    """Calculate NSFR."""
    calculate_asf_C13()
    calculate_rsf_C13()
    if ws_nsfr_required > Decimal('0'):
        ws_nsfr_ratio: Decimal = (ws_nsfr_available / ws_nsfr_required) * Decimal('100')

def calculate_asf_C13() -> None:
    """Calculate Available Stable Funding (ASF)."""
    ws_nsfr_available: Decimal = Decimal('0')
    ws_nsfr_available += ws_tier1_capital
    ws_nsfr_available += ws_tier2_capital
    ws_stable_funding: Decimal = ws_retail_deposits * Decimal('0.95') + ws_wholesale_deposits_1yr * Decimal('1.00') + ws_wholesale_deposits_6m * Decimal('0.50')
    ws_nsfr_available += ws_stable_funding

def calculate_rsf_C13() -> None:
    """Calculate Required Stable Funding (RSF)."""
    ws_nsfr_required: Decimal = Decimal('0')
    ws_required_stable: Decimal = ws_cash_position * Decimal('0.00') + ws_govt_securities * Decimal('0.05') + ws_corporate_bonds * Decimal('0.50') + ws_residential_mortgages * Decimal('0.65') + ws_commercial_loans * Decimal('0.85')
    ws_nsfr_required += ws_required_stable

def calculate_basic_ratio_C13() -> None:
    """Calculate basic liquidity ratio."""
    if ws_total_deposits > Decimal('0'):
        ws_liquidity_ratio: Decimal = (ws_liquid_assets / ws_total_deposits) * Decimal('100')

def monitor_liquidity_limits_C13() -> None:
    """Monitor liquidity limits and trigger actions."""
    if ws_lcr_ratio < Decimal('100'):
        lcr_breach_action_C13()
    if ws_nsfr_ratio < Decimal('100'):
        nsfr_breach_action_C13()
    if ws_liquidity_ratio < ws_internal_limit:
        internal_breach_action_C13()

def lcr_breach_action_C13() -> None:
    """Take action upon LCR breach."""
    ws_alert_type: str = 'LCR BREACH'
    send_liquidity_alert_C13()
    initiate_remediation_C13()

def nsfr_breach_action_C13() -> None:
    """Take action upon NSFR breach."""
    ws_alert_type: str = 'NSFR BREACH'
    send_liquidity_alert_C13()

def internal_breach_action_C13() -> None:
    """Take action upon internal limit breach."""
    ws_alert_type: str = 'INTERNAL LIMIT BREACH'
    send_liquidity_alert_C13()

def send_liquidity_alert_C13() -> None:
    """Send liquidity alert."""
    ws_notif_type: str = 'liquidity_alert'
    ws_notif_channel: str = 'EMAIL'
    ws_notif_subject: str = f"URGENT: {ws_alert_type}"
    send_notification_C13()

def initiate_remediation_C13() -> None:
    """Initiate remediation actions for LCR breach."""
    invest_excess_reserves_C13()
    sell_fed_funds_C13()

def contingency_funding_plan_C13() -> None:
    """Execute contingency funding plan."""
    assess_stress_scenario_C13()
    identify_funding_sources_C13()
    update_cfp_document_C13()

def assess_stress_scenario_C13() -> None:
    """Assess stress scenario and estimate deposit runoff."""
    if ws_stress_level == 'LOW':
        ws_deposit_runoff: Decimal = Decimal('0.05')
    elif ws_stress_level == 'MEDIUM':
        ws_deposit_runoff: Decimal = Decimal('0.15')
    elif ws_stress_level == 'HIGH':
        ws_deposit_runoff: Decimal = Decimal('0.30')
    elif ws_stress_level == 'SEVERE':
        ws_deposit_runoff: Decimal = Decimal('0.50')
    ws_stressed_outflows: Decimal = ws_total_deposits * ws_deposit_runoff

def identify_funding_sources_C13() -> None:
    """Identify available funding sources."""
    ws_available_funding: Decimal = Decimal('0')
    ws_available_funding += ws_fhlb_capacity
    ws_available_funding += ws_repo_capacity
    ws_available_funding += ws_fed_discount_window
    ws_available_funding += ws_asset_sale_capacity
    if ws_available_funding < ws_stressed_outflows:
        ws_cfp_status: str = 'INADEQUATE'
    else:
        ws_cfp_status: str = 'ADEQUATE'

def update_cfp_document_C13() -> None:
    """Update contingency funding plan document with current assessment."""
    pass

def read_vault_cash_file_C13() -> str:
    """Read vault cash file."""
    pass

    """Get vault balance from record."""
    pass

    """Read fed account file."""

    """Read correspondent file."""

def get_corr_balance_C13(ws_corr_rec: str) -> Decimal:
    """Get correspondent balance from record."""

    """Read loan schedule file."""

def update_cfp_document_C14() -> None:
    """Updates CFP document."""
    global WS_CFP_UPDATE_DATE_C14, CFP_OVERALL_STATUS_C14, CFP_TOTAL_SOURCES_C14, CFP_STRESS_NEEDS_C14
    WS_CFP_UPDATE_DATE_C14 = str(datetime.date.today())
    CFP_OVERALL_STATUS_C14  = None  # TODO: was WS_CFP_STATUS_C14
    CFP_TOTAL_SOURCES_C14 = WS_AVAILABLE_FUNDING_C14
    CFP_STRESS_NEEDS_C14 = WS_STRESSED_OUTFLOWS_C14
    pass

def capital_management_C14() -> None:
    """Capital management procedures."""
    calculate_capital_ratios_C14()
    risk_weighted_assets_C14()
    capital_planning_C14()
    stress_testing_C14()

def calculate_capital_ratios_C14() -> None:
    """Calculates capital ratios."""
    calculate_tier1_C14()
    calculate_tier2_C14()
    calculate_ratios_C14()

def calculate_tier1_C14() -> None:
    """Calculates Tier 1 capital."""
    global WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 += None  # TODO: was WS_COMMON_STOCK_C14
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 += WS_RETAINED_EARNINGS_C14
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 += None  # TODO: was WS_AOCI_C14
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 -= None  # TODO: was WS_GOODWILL_C14
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 -= None  # TODO: was WS_INTANGIBLES_C14
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 -= WS_DTA_DEDUCTION_C14

def calculate_tier2_C14() -> None:
    """Calculates Tier 2 capital."""
    global WS_TIER2_CAPITAL_C14, WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL_C14
    WS_TIER2_CAPITAL_C14 = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_TIER2_CAPITAL_C14 += None  # TODO: was WS_SUB_DEBT_C14
    WS_TIER2_CAPITAL_C14 += WS_ALLL_ELIGIBLE_C14
    WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL_C14 = WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 + WS_TIER2_CAPITAL_C14

def calculate_ratios_C14() -> None:
    """Calculates capital ratios."""
    global WS_CET1_RATIO_C14, WS_CAPITAL_RATIO_C14, WS_LEVERAGE_RATIO_C14
    if WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 > 0:
        WS_CET1_RATIO_C14 = (WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 / WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14) * 100
        WS_CAPITAL_RATIO_C14 = (WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL_C14 / WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14) * 100
    if WS_TOTAL_ASSETS_C14 > 0:
        WS_LEVERAGE_RATIO_C14 = (WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 / WS_TOTAL_ASSETS_C14) * 100

def risk_weighted_assets_C14() -> None:
    """Calculates risk-weighted assets."""
    global WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 = Decimal("0.00")
    credit_rwa_C14()
    market_rwa_C14()
    operational_rwa_C14()

def credit_rwa_C14() -> None:
    """Calculates credit risk-weighted assets."""
    global WS_CASH_RWA_C14, WS_GOVT_RWA_C14, WS_BANK_RWA_C14, WS_MORTGAGE_RWA_C14, WS_COMMERCIAL_RWA_C14, WS_CONSUMER_RWA_C14, WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14
    WS_CASH_RWA_C14 = WS_CASH_POSITION_C14 * Decimal("0.00")
    WS_GOVT_RWA_C14 = WS_GOVT_SECURITIES_C14 * Decimal("0.00")
    WS_BANK_RWA_C14 = WS_BANK_DEPOSITS_C14 * Decimal("0.20")
    WS_MORTGAGE_RWA_C14 = WS_RESIDENTIAL_MORTGAGES_C14 * Decimal("0.50")
    WS_COMMERCIAL_RWA_C14 = WS_COMMERCIAL_LOANS_C14 * Decimal("1.00")
    WS_CONSUMER_RWA_C14 = WS_CONSUMER_LOANS_C14 * Decimal("1.00")
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 += None  # TODO: was WS_CASH_RWA_C14
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 += None  # TODO: was WS_GOVT_RWA_C14
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 += None  # TODO: was WS_BANK_RWA_C14
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 += None  # TODO: was WS_MORTGAGE_RWA_C14
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 += WS_COMMERCIAL_RWA_C14
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 += None  # TODO: was WS_CONSUMER_RWA_C14

def market_rwa_C14() -> None:
    """Calculates market risk-weighted assets."""
    global WS_MARKET_RWA_C14, WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14
    WS_MARKET_RWA_C14 = WS_TRADING_ASSETS_C14 * WS_MARKET_RISK_FACTOR_C14
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 += None  # TODO: was WS_MARKET_RWA_C14

def operational_rwa_C14() -> None:
    """Calculates operational risk-weighted assets."""
    global WS_OPERATIONAL_RWA_C14, WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14
    WS_OPERATIONAL_RWA_C14 = WS_GROSS_INCOME_C14 * WS_OPERATIONAL_FACTOR_C14 * Decimal("12.5")
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 += WS_OPERATIONAL_RWA_C14

def capital_planning_C14() -> None:
    """Performs capital planning."""
    project_capital_needs_C14()
    identify_capital_actions_C14()
    update_capital_plan_C14()

def project_capital_needs_C14() -> None:
    """Projects capital needs."""
    global WS_PROJECTED_RWA_C14, WS_REQUIRED_CAPITAL_C14, WS_CAPITAL_GAP_C14
    WS_PROJECTED_RWA_C14 = WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 * (1 + WS_GROWTH_RATE_C14)
    WS_REQUIRED_CAPITAL_C14 = WS_PROJECTED_RWA_C14 * WS_TARGET_RATIO_C14 / 100
    WS_CAPITAL_GAP_C14 = WS_REQUIRED_CAPITAL_C14 - WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL_C14

def identify_capital_actions_C14() -> None:
    """Identifies necessary capital actions."""
    global WS_CAPITAL_ACTION_C14
    if WS_CAPITAL_GAP_C14 > 0:
        if WS_CAPITAL_GAP_C14 <= WS_RETAINED_EARNINGS_PROJ_C14:
            WS_CAPITAL_ACTION_C14 = 'ORGANIC GROWTH'
        elif WS_CAPITAL_GAP_C14 <= WS_SUB_DEBT_CAPACITY_C14:
            WS_CAPITAL_ACTION_C14 = 'SUB DEBT ISSUANCE'
        else:
            WS_CAPITAL_ACTION_C14 = 'EQUITY RAISE'
    else:
        WS_CAPITAL_ACTION_C14 = 'NO ACTION NEEDED'

def update_capital_plan_C14() -> None:
    """Updates the capital plan."""
    global WS_PLAN_UPDATE_DATE_C14, PLAN_RECOMMENDED_ACTION_C14, PLAN_GAP_AMOUNT_C14
    WS_PLAN_UPDATE_DATE_C14 = str(datetime.date.today())
    PLAN_RECOMMENDED_ACTION_C14 = WS_CAPITAL_ACTION_C14
    PLAN_GAP_AMOUNT_C14  = None  # TODO: was WS_CAPITAL_GAP_C14
    pass

def stress_testing_C14() -> None:
    """Performs stress testing."""
    run_baseline_C14()
    run_adverse_C14()
    run_severely_adverse_C14()
    compile_results_C14()

def run_baseline_C14() -> None:
    """Runs the baseline stress test scenario."""
    global WS_SCENARIO_NAME_C14, WS_RATE_SHOCK_C14, WS_GDP_CHANGE_C14, WS_UNEMPLOYMENT_RATE_C14, WS_HOUSING_DECLINE_C14
    WS_SCENARIO_NAME_C14 = 'BASELINE'
    WS_RATE_SHOCK_C14 = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_GDP_CHANGE_C14 = Decimal("2.50")
    WS_UNEMPLOYMENT_RATE_C14 = Decimal("4.00")
    WS_HOUSING_DECLINE_C14 = Decimal("0.00")
    calculate_stress_impact_C14()

def run_adverse_C14() -> None:
    """Runs the adverse stress test scenario."""
    global WS_SCENARIO_NAME_C14, WS_RATE_SHOCK_C14, WS_GDP_CHANGE_C14, WS_UNEMPLOYMENT_RATE_C14, WS_HOUSING_DECLINE_C14
    WS_SCENARIO_NAME_C14 = 'ADVERSE'
    WS_RATE_SHOCK_C14 = Decimal("2.00")
    WS_GDP_CHANGE_C14 = Decimal("-1.50")
    WS_UNEMPLOYMENT_RATE_C14 = Decimal("7.00")
    WS_HOUSING_DECLINE_C14 = Decimal("-15.00")
    calculate_stress_impact_C14()

def run_severely_adverse_C14() -> None:
    """Runs the severely adverse stress test scenario."""
    global WS_SCENARIO_NAME_C14, WS_RATE_SHOCK_C14, WS_GDP_CHANGE_C14, WS_UNEMPLOYMENT_RATE_C14, WS_HOUSING_DECLINE_C14
    WS_SCENARIO_NAME_C14 = 'severely_adverse'
    WS_RATE_SHOCK_C14 = Decimal("3.00")
    WS_GDP_CHANGE_C14 = Decimal("-6.00")
    WS_UNEMPLOYMENT_RATE_C14 = Decimal("10.00")
    WS_HOUSING_DECLINE_C14 = Decimal("-30.00")
    calculate_stress_impact_C14()

def compile_results_C14() -> None:
    """Compiles the stress test results."""
    if WS_STRESS_PASS_FAIL_C14 == 'FAIL':
        remediation_actions_C14()
    print('STRESS TEST RESULTS COMPILED')

def calculate_stress_impact_C14() -> None:
    """Calculates the impact of a stress test scenario."""
    global WS_CREDIT_LOSSES_C14, WS_MARKET_LOSSES_C14, WS_STRESS_LOSSES_C14, WS_STRESSED_CAPITAL_C14, WS_STRESSED_RATIO_C14, WS_STRESS_PASS_FAIL_C14, WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL2_C14
    WS_CREDIT_LOSSES_C14 = WS_LOAN_PORTFOLIO_C14 * WS_STRESS_LGD_C14 * WS_STRESS_PD_C14
    WS_MARKET_LOSSES_C14 = WS_TRADING_ASSETS_C14 * WS_RATE_SHOCK_C14 / 100
    WS_STRESS_LOSSES_C14 = WS_CREDIT_LOSSES_C14 + WS_MARKET_LOSSES_C14
    WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL2_C14 = WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL_C14
    WS_STRESSED_CAPITAL_C14 = WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL2_C14 - WS_STRESS_LOSSES_C14
    WS_STRESSED_RATIO_C14 = (WS_STRESSED_CAPITAL_C14 / WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14) * 100
    if WS_STRESSED_RATIO_C14 >= WS_MIN_CAPITAL_RATIO_C14:
        WS_STRESS_PASS_FAIL_C14 = 'PASS'
    else:
        WS_STRESS_PASS_FAIL_C14 = 'FAIL'

def remediation_actions_C14() -> None:
    """Takes remediation actions after a failed stress test."""
    global WS_NOTIF_TYPE_C14, WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL_C14, WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT_C14
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE_C14 = 'stress_failure'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL_C14 = 'EMAIL'
    WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT_C14 = 'URGENT: Stress test failure - action required'
    send_notification_C14()

    if WS_RECON_DIFFC15 != 0:

def load_ic_balances_C15() -> None:
    """Loads intercompany balances from a file."""
    global WS_IC_COUNTC15, WS_EOF_FLAGC15, WS_IC_BALANCEC15, WS_IC_ARRAYC15
    WS_IC_COUNTC15 = 0
    WS_EOF_FLAGC15 = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAGC15 != 'Y':
        # READ intercompany_file INTO ws_ic_balance (replace with file read)
        WS_IC_BALANCEC15 = "READ_INTERCOMPANY_FILE"  # Placeholder for file read
        if WS_IC_BALANCEC15 == "EOF":
            WS_EOF_FLAGC15 = 'Y'
        else:
            WS_IC_COUNTC15 += 1
            if WS_IC_BALANCEC15 is not None:
                WS_IC_ARRAYC15.append(WS_IC_BALANCEC15) # Assuming array append works
    WS_EOF_FLAGC15 = 'N'

def match_ic_pairs_C15() -> None:
    """Matches intercompany pairs."""
    global WS_IC_IDXC15, WS_IC_COUNTC15
    WS_IC_IDXC15 = 1
    while WS_IC_IDXC15 <= WS_IC_COUNTC15:
        find_ic_counterpart_C15()
        WS_IC_IDXC15 += 1

def archive_audit_logs_C15() -> None:
    """Archives audit logs."""
    global WS_END_OF_MONTHC15
    if WS_END_OF_MONTHC15 == 'Y':
        move_to_archive_C15()
        compress_archive_C15()

def move_to_archive_C15() -> None:
    """Moves audit logs to the archive."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAGC15, WS_AUDIT_RECORDC15, WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMPC15, WS_ARCHIVE_DATEC15
    WS_EOF_FLAGC15 = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAGC15 != 'Y':
        # READ audit_file INTO ws_audit_record (replace with file read)
        WS_AUDIT_RECORDC15 = "READ_AUDIT_FILE"  # Placeholder for file read
        if WS_AUDIT_RECORDC15 == "EOF":
            WS_EOF_FLAGC15 = 'Y'
        else:
            if WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMPC15 is not None and WS_ARCHIVE_DATEC15 is not None and WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMPC15 < WS_ARCHIVE_DATEC15:
                # WRITE archive_audit_record FROM ws_audit_record (replace with file write)
                print("Writing archive_audit_record")  # Placeholder for file write
                # DELETE audit_file (replace with file delete)
                print("Deleting audit_file")  # Placeholder for file delete
    WS_EOF_FLAGC15 = 'N'

def cpu_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """Collects CPU metrics."""
    global WS_CPU_UTILIZATIONC15, WS_CPU_ALERTC15
    # CALL 'GETCPU' USING ws_cpu_utilization (replace with Python equivalent)
    WS_CPU_UTILIZATIONC15 = 81 # Placeholder

    if WS_CPU_UTILIZATIONC15 is not None and WS_CPU_UTILIZATIONC15 > 80:
        WS_CPU_ALERTC15 = 'Y'

def memory_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """Collects memory metrics."""
    global WS_MEMORY_UTILIZATIONC15, WS_MEMORY_ALERTC15
    # CALL 'GETMEM' USING ws_memory_utilization (replace with Python equivalent)
    WS_MEMORY_UTILIZATIONC15 = 86 # Placeholder
    if WS_MEMORY_UTILIZATIONC15 is not None and WS_MEMORY_UTILIZATIONC15 > 85:
        WS_MEMORY_ALERTC15 = 'Y'

def io_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """Collects I/O metrics."""
    global WS_IO_WAIT_TIMEC15, WS_IO_THRESHOLDC15, WS_IO_ALERTC15
    # CALL 'GETIO' USING ws_io_wait_time (replace with Python equivalent)
    WS_IO_WAIT_TIMEC15 = 101 # Placeholder
    WS_IO_THRESHOLDC15 = 100
    if WS_IO_WAIT_TIMEC15 is not None and WS_IO_THRESHOLDC15 is not None and WS_IO_WAIT_TIMEC15 > WS_IO_THRESHOLDC15:
        WS_IO_ALERTC15 = 'Y'

def transaction_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """Collects transaction metrics."""
    global WS_TPSC15, WS_TRANS_COUNTC15, WS_ELAPSED_SECONDSC15, WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIMEC15, WS_AVG_RESPONSEC15
    WS_TPSC15 = WS_TRANS_COUNTC15 / WS_ELAPSED_SECONDSC15 if WS_ELAPSED_SECONDSC15 != 0 else 0
    WS_AVG_RESPONSEC15 = WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIMEC15 / WS_TRANS_COUNTC15 if WS_TRANS_COUNTC15 != 0 else 0

def analyze_performance_C15() -> None:
    """Analyzes performance metrics."""
    global WS_AVG_RESPONSEC15, WS_RESPONSE_THRESHOLDC15, WS_PERF_DEGRADEDC15, WS_TPSC15, WS_MIN_TPS_THRESHOLDC15, WS_THROUGHPUT_LOWC15
    WS_RESPONSE_THRESHOLDC15 = 10
    WS_MIN_TPS_THRESHOLDC15 = 5
    if WS_AVG_RESPONSEC15 is not None and WS_RESPONSE_THRESHOLDC15 is not None and WS_AVG_RESPONSEC15 > WS_RESPONSE_THRESHOLDC15:
        WS_PERF_DEGRADEDC15 = 'Y'
    if WS_TPSC15 is not None and WS_MIN_TPS_THRESHOLDC15 is not None and WS_TPSC15 < WS_MIN_TPS_THRESHOLDC15:
        WS_THROUGHPUT_LOWC15 = 'Y'

def generate_alerts_C15() -> None:
    """Generates performance alerts."""
    global WS_CPU_ALERTC15, WS_MEMORY_ALERTC15, WS_PERF_DEGRADEDC15
    if WS_CPU_ALERTC15 == 'Y':
        send_cpu_alert_C15()
    if WS_MEMORY_ALERTC15 == 'Y':
        send_memory_alert_C15()
    if WS_PERF_DEGRADEDC15 == 'Y':
        send_perf_alert_C15()

def send_cpu_alert_C15() -> None:
    """Sends a CPU utilization alert."""

    WS_NOTIF_TYPEC15 = 'high_cpu'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNELC15 = 'EMAIL'

    if WS_CPU_UTILIZATIONC15 is not None:
        WS_NOTIF_SUBJECTC15 = f'ALERT: CPU utilization at {WS_CPU_UTILIZATIONC15}%'
    eldef send_cpu_alert_C15() -> None:
    """Sends a CPU utilization alert."""
    global WS_NOTIF_TYPEC15, WS_NOTIF_CHANNELC15, WS_NOTIF_SUBJECTC15
    WS_NOTIF_TYPEC15 = 'high_cpu'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNELC15 = 'EMAIL'

    send_notification_C15()

def full_backup_C15() -> None:
    """Performs a full database backup."""
    global WS_DAY_OF_WEEKC15, WS_BACKUP_STATUSC15, WS_LAST_FULL_BACKUPC15
    WS_DAY_OF_WEEKC15 = 7
    if WS_DAY_OF_WEEKC15 == 7:
        # CALL 'FULLBKUP' USING ws_backup_status (replace with Python equivalent)
        WS_BACKUP_STATUSC15 = 'SUCCESS'  # Placeholder
        if WS_BACKUP_STATUSC15 == 'SUCCESS':
            WS_LAST_FULL_BACKUPC15 = "CURRENT_DATE"  # Placeholder
    else:
        pass

def incremental_backup_C15() -> None:

    WS_BACKUP_STATUSC15 = 'SUCCESS'  # Placeholder

def verify_backup_C15() -> None:
    """Verifies the database backup."""
    global WS_VERIFY_STATUSC15, WS_NOTIF_TYPEC15
    # CALL 'VERIFYBK' USING ws_verify_status (replace with Python equivalent)
    WS_VERIFY_STATUSC15 = 'FAILED'  # Placeholder
    if WS_VERIFY_STATUSC15 != 'SUCCESS':
        WS_NOTIF_TYPEC15 = 'backup_failed'
        send_notification_C15()
    else:
        pass
